## Asset manifest — GREAT HALL 3D (Mode C cinematic "Große Halle" reveal, shot S011 / LS_M1_Hall)

| | |
|---|---|
| Area owner | hall (great_hall_3d specialist) — round 2 "inhabited, monumental, symbolically charged" |
| Date | 2026-11-04 (round 1 afternoon, round 2 evening), 2026-11-05 round 3 (hall-fix: user playtest feedback) |
| Engine | Godot 4.7.2-stable, Forward+, GDScript |
| Provenance | **100 % original.** Geometry is generated procedurally at runtime by GDScript (SurfaceTool); all 2D textures/sprites are generated by the deterministic Python generators below (Python 3.13, Pillow + numpy, on top of `tools/pixelgen/pg.py`, which is not modified). No downloads, no third-party models/textures, no AI image models, no Blender files, nothing traced from `documentation/references/`. Two runs of all generators produce byte-identical PNGs (verified by md5 over all 84 files). |
| Licence | Project-owned original work (same terms as the game's own code/art). No third-party rights. |
| Content policy | TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.4 (user request of round 2, overrides the older "no swastika" lines in ART_DIRECTION §4 / WORLD_BIBLE): every image with a real regime emblem exists as a neutral file `name.png` (fictional **Reichssiegel**: white obelisk in a black disc on grey-black cloth; eagle holding the Reichssiegel) and `name_hist.png` (red banner, white disc, black swastika on point; eagle holding the swastika wreath). Every loader resolves through `Symbols.pick()` at build time (setting `historical_symbols`, CLI `--symbols=0|1`). The insignia are in-world iconography of the regime in a critical scene: the composition keeps the **evicted family** at the sealed door as the warm focal point, the regime's banner hangs over their sealed door, a neighbour draws her curtains and turns the light off. The red is pushed into the palette's dark terracotta (no saturated "reward" red), the emblems are never UI, rewards or decoration. The **eagle above the globe** on the lantern follows Speer's real 1939 design for the Große Halle (the eagle originally planned above a swastika was changed to the globe) and carries no swastika, so it has no `_hist` variant. The Große Halle itself is fictional as a completed building (alternate 1964). |
| Reproduce | `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_great_hall.py` (sky/fog/smoke, placeholders, door, drawing, posters, **writes all `.png.import` files**: lossless, `detect_3d/compress_to=0`, mipmaps only for 3D-quad textures) · `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_great_hall_symbols.py` · `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_great_hall_life.py` · then `"$GODOT" --headless --path . --import` (holding the shared `.import_lock`) |
| Visual test | `"$GODOT" --path C:/Spiele/AshesOfEmpires res://tests/visual/HallPreview.tscn -- [--symbols=0|1] [--still=1,4,7,10,12] [--size=642x362]` (windowed; captures to `_preview/`, prints frame/GPU/CPU/update-cost statistics when playing in real time) |
| Logic test | `"$GODOT" --headless --path C:/Spiele/AshesOfEmpires res://tests/visual/HallPreview.tscn -- --selftest` (exit 0 = pass) |

### Round 3 (2026-11-05, hall-fix) — user playtest feedback
User (verbatim, abridged): "links am Gebäude eine Flagge … hängt perspektivisch falsch", "der Platz scheint im vorderen
Bereich überladen, mehr nach hinten verteilt", "die Szene soll länger gehen, da es ein imposanter Auftritt ist",
"die Grafik scheint etwas anstrengender geworden … nur leicht reduzieren".
* **Corner banner** (`hall_builder._build_street_flags` + new `_banner_flat`): the banner over the family's sealed door
  used to be a camera-facing quad on a rod 0.8 m off the 45° chamfer — an upright rectangle in front of a
  foreshortened facade. It now hangs **flat in the chamfer's plane** (0.3 m in front of the wall, rod + two wall
  brackets) and converges with the window lintels. Same texture/`Symbols.pick` (swastika in `_hist`, Reichssiegel
  otherwise). Street outriggers (right block) and cross-street banners were already correct.
* **Plaza front thinned, life moved back**: queue 5 → 3 (+ the woman who joins at ~13 s), the two other near walkers
  (walk_36) removed from the plaza edge; ring walkers (walk_24) now loop on 74 m paths whose ends lie outside the
  visible wedge (no stop/pop during the longer run); mid plaza walk_14 6 → 10 (106 m loops), far plaza walk_8
  18 → 28; the patrol starts off-frame and marches across the mid plaza from ~10.9 s to the end. Sprites 102 → 112.
* **Slightly calmer picture**: screen-space snow density 0.55 → 0.45.
* **24 s instead of 11 s** (`hall_camera_rig.keys`, `hall_reveal` SHOTS/CUES/`lock_from` 5.5/`window_close_at` 19.6):
  h1 0–5.5 s the same crane 1.83× slower · h2 5.5–14.5 s long establishing hold (camera nearly stops, lens shift 62)
  while the life plays out: tram bell + departure 6.4 s, car on the plaza 8.9–14.5 s, loudspeaker 8.6 s, pigeons
  8.75 s, lorry 10.6–19.6 s, patrol 10.9 s, dark saloon 12.3–15.1 s, queue arrival 13.2 s · h3 14.5–21.5 s slow
  low-angle push (1.5 m forward, 0.3 m down) with a **tilt up** (shift 62 → 90), loudspeaker 15.2 s, neighbour
  closes the curtains 19.6 s, light out 20.6 s · 21.5–24 s held final frame. The final frame keeps x/z of round 2
  (facade calibration unchanged) and is framed for the game's **2.39:1 letterbox** (`fade.letterbox`, bars hide world
  rows 0–45 / 315–361): crowning eagle top ≈ row 48, the family's feet ≈ row 303 — in round 2 the bars hid the
  eagle and the lantern in-game. h1-only timings (kiosk customer/halo, near smoke, babushka) scale with the crane.
* API unchanged (`play/skip/finished once/cue/seek/get_time/get_duration/set_fade/set_people_sprites`);
  `hall_people.ref_time` (= `lock_from`) replaces the hard-coded 3.0 s calibration time.
* Locale: `hall.ad.h2`/`hall.ad.h3` audio descriptions mention the long hold and the tilt up to the eagle; cue
  strings unchanged.

**Verification round 3** (RTX 4090; note: a full-screen game was using the GPU at 99 % during these runs):
harness stills at t = 2/3/5.5/7/11/16/18/19.9/20.5/23.5 s in both symbol modes, 35–36 colours per frame, **0 px outside
`pg.PALETTES['berlin']`** (12 final frames `_preview/final_r3_s{0,1}_*`); real-time run (s1, 642×362): frame delta avg
6.2 ms / p50 5.6 / p95 8.3 ms (GPU-contended; level GPU 0.18 ms, viewport CPU 0.30 ms, level update avg 0.81 ms,
max 2.2 ms); `--selftest` PASS (finished once, 7 cues in order, locale keys, overrides, viewport restore);
`tests/visual/camera_hall_rig_check.gd` PASS; in-game `-- --cinematic=LS_M1_Hall --capture=… --frames=3000/3550`
(1920×1080, letterboxed): the reveal runs, story beat and final framing as above, engine FPS 175–198.

### Files

#### Scene, code, shaders
| Path | Role |

| `scripts/levels/hall/hall_reveal.gd` | Root script / public API (unchanged): `signal finished` (exactly once), `play()`, `skip()`, `set_people_sprites(paths)`, `signal cue(key)`, `seek(t)`, `set_paused(p)`, `set_fade(a)`, `get_time()`, `get_duration()`, `get_shot_id()`, `is_playing()`, level shims. New: `life` node, per-frame update cost in `perf` (µs), story beat (neighbour → curtains → light out), cues `hall.cue.pigeons` (3.75 s) and `hall.cue.patrol` (5.2 s). |
| `scripts/levels/hall/hall_builder.gd` | Procedural set (≈ 26 k triangles in 24 meshes, built in ≈ 75 ms). Round 2 adds: facade ornament (triangular pediments on the piano nobile, keystones, cornice consoles), people behind lit curtains (silhouette atlas), the regime's banners (3 giant portico banners at 1 texel/px, 2×3 flank masts, plaza masts, outrigger banners along the street, the banner over the family's door — all via `Symbols.pick`), the state eagle on the portico and the crowning eagle on the lantern (sprites), the tram as its own moving node, tram stop island with stop sign and queue, newspaper kiosk, manholes, the sweeper's cleared strip with heaps of swept snow, footprint trails, three tower cranes + scaffolding at the wings, far city lights, smoking factory stacks with blinking warning lights, the parked official car, cold mercury lamps, an eagle floodlight pair and a cold people fill light. |
| `scripts/levels/hall/hall_life.gd` | **New.** Street life as a pure function of time: walkers in five distance bands, a woman who joins the queue, the marching patrol, sweeper + cart, pigeons (startled by the loudspeaker, fly off to the hall), crows on the cable, a flock wheeling in front of the dome, chimney smoke, manhole steam, the family's belongings (chair, crate, bedding roll, birdcage with a canary), a newspaper page tumbling across the road, the departing tram (accelerates along the curve), a grey saloon (crane shot), a dark saloon crossing the ring, a covered lorry, a far tram, moving head lights (SpotLight3D) and the lamp halos sent to the post shader every frame. |
| `scripts/levels/hall/hall_people.gd` | Sprite actors (extended, same API): animated sheet rows (character `sheet.json` anims or life sheets), paths with arclength, **feet-planted walk cycles** (frame = distance walked in sprite pixels / ground speed), facing by screen direction, visibility windows, dithered dissolve, `always_lock` / `no_lock`, `pos_fn` / `frame_fn` callables. Texel lock from 3 s: 1 texel = 1 screen pixel, feet on whole pixels. The family now plays its `idle_side` rows (breathing). |
| `scripts/levels/hall/hall_mesh_kit.gd` | SurfaceTool helper; new `quad_uv()` (atlas regions). |
| `scripts/levels/hall/hall_camera_rig.gd` | Camera path (render-camera's smooth lens shift kept: whole low-res px + remainder via `Game.view.set_fraction`). Round 3: 24 s key table (see below). |
| `assets/shaders/pixel3d_post.gdshader` | Post quad: + **lamp halos** (`halo_count`, `halo_a[32]`, `halo_b[32]`: screen pos/depth/radius + colour/strength; additive in sRGB, only behind the lamp's depth, turned into dithered rings by the palette snap). |
| `assets/shaders/pixel3d_sprite.gdshader` | Sprites: + `unshaded` (smoke, steam, birds), + `dissolve` (ordered-dither fade / translucency). |
| `assets/shaders/pixel3d_toon.gdshader` | Unchanged (banners use per-vertex emission via UV2.x). |
| `tests/visual/HallPreview.tscn` + `hall_preview.gd` | Harness: default captures t = 2, 7, 11, 16, 20.5, 23.5 (round 3); reports the level's own update cost and sprite counts; selftest playback guard is wall-clock (30 s) instead of 4000 frames. |
| `data/locale/de_hall.json` | 7 cue strings (+ pigeons, patrol; tram bell and window cue rewritten) and the 3 audio-description lines rewritten for the new life and symbols. |

### Verification (2026-11-04 evening, RTX 4090, Windows 11, 240 Hz display)
* Real-time harness runs at 642×362 in **both symbol modes**: frame delta avg 4.27–4.29 ms (vsync-bound ≈ 234 fps, p95 ≤ 4.8 ms; spikes 70–88 ms are the PNG-saving frames), viewport GPU 0.19 ms, viewport CPU 0.24–0.26 ms, 141 draw calls, ≈ 86 k primitives, **level update (camera + 102 sprites + life + halos) avg 0.64–0.66 ms, max 1.28 ms**.
* Palette: every captured frame (10 final frames + 6 story-beat frames) uses 35–36 colours, **0 pixels outside `pg.PALETTES['berlin']`**; every opaque texel of every generated symbol/life/sky/fog texture is a berlin colour.
* `--selftest` (headless): PASS (finished once for skip/skip and full play, all 7 cues in order, locale keys present, overrides, viewport restore). `tests/run_all.gd`: 17 files, 130 cases, ALL PASSED.
* Looked at in 3× crops: family + door banner + story window sequence (8.6 → 10.7 s), queue/sweeper/guard/steam, portico eagle and banners in both modes, lantern eagle, crane shot (pediments, kiosk, parked car, street banners), motion strips (walk cycles advance with distance, sweeper sweeps, tram departs, pigeons take off).

### Known limitations
* The generated citizens are background extras (36 px and smaller); the 46 px class uses the character sheets (`clerk`, `babushka`, family, `guard`) and follows their redesigns automatically. A 64 px foreground walker was tried and rejected (too crude at that size).
* The kiosk is only seen in the crane shot (h1); flying pigeons scale with perspective (no texel lock) while they fly away.
* Round 2 was not run inside `Main.tscn`; round 3 was captured in-game via `--cinematic=LS_M1_Hall` (no autoplay/inputplay).
* The h2 hold (lens shift 62) still hides the lantern eagle under the in-game letterbox; it is revealed by the h3 tilt. If
  the lead drops the bars for this cinematic, the frames are composed for the full 16:9 picture as well.
* The top rows of the sky matte (painted cirrus streaks) become visible above the eagle in the un-letterboxed final frame.
* `data/v3/cinematics.json` LS_M1_Hall still lists the old 3/5/3 s shot durations (canonical data, not owned by hall).

## Asset manifest part — parade (L_M1_Parade, 2026-11-05)

Second Great Hall cinematic, played right after `L_M1_HallReveal`: later that day the regime stages a military
parade on the plaza in front of the Great Hall. Mode C (3D pixel render, low-res world path), palette `berlin`,
47 s, 8 shots with hard cuts. All assets are original and generated deterministically by the scripts below
(licence: project-owned, no third-party material, no downloads).

### Scene and code
| Path | Content |
|---|---|
| `scenes/levels/L_M1_Parade.tscn` | root `Node3D` (script `parade_level.gd`), exports `level_id` L_M1_Parade, `mode` 3d, `protagonist` none, `grade_preset` hall_3d, `ambience` amb_berlin_street, `music` mus_parade |
| `scripts/levels/parade/parade_level.gd` | API like the reveal: `signal finished` (once), `signal cue(key)`, `play/skip/seek/set_paused/set_fade/get_time/get_duration/get_shot_id`; reuses `hall_builder.gd`, `hall_people.gd`, `hall_camera_rig.gd`, `pixel3d_post.gdshader` read-only; `own_sfx` plays the `parade.sfx.*` cues via `Audio.sfx("parade_*")` |
| `scripts/levels/parade/parade_shots.gd` | shot table p1–p8 (un-rotated camera, lens-shift tilts, eased moves, hard cuts) |
| `scripts/levels/parade/parade_set.gd` | tribune, barrier, loudspeakers, 27 searchlight pillars, 7 vehicles (4 tanks, 3 half-tracks), 5 jets, MultiMesh crowd (~13.9k), formations (1.8k), 6 marching blocks (720), guards, officials, dust puffs, cold spot lights |
| `assets/architecture/parade/parade_figures.gdshader` | MultiMesh billboard shader: per-instance LOD strip chosen from the projected height, feet snapped to whole pixels, 1 texel = 1 px (largest strip may scale ×2–3 for foreground silhouettes), toon light, flag 0.55 |

### Generated art — `tools/pixelgen/gen_parade.py` (`py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_parade.py`)
| File | Content |
|---|---|
| `assets/architecture/parade/figures.png` + `figures.json` | 1368×399 atlas, 13 LOD strips (figure height 2–104 px) × 18 slots: marching soldier profile (4 frames), soldier front, guard front, 6 civilians from behind, child with limp flag, 2 grey officials, 3 dust puffs. Faces of troops = shadow band (anonymous) |
| `assets/architecture/parade/flag_limp.png` / `flag_limp_hist.png` | the child's limp hand flag (neutral cloth / historical variant via `Symbols.pick`) |
| `assets/architecture/parade/sky_parade.png`, `fog_parade.png` | 1024×720 cold overcast dusk matte and cold fog gradient (same screen mapping as the hall's sky, anchor 512,430) |

Insignia (§12.4): tribune, portico and march banners use the hall builder's Symbols-resolved materials
(`banner_portico`, `flag_street`); neutral mode shows the fictional Reichssiegel. No SS runes, no salutes.

### Generated audio — `tools/audiogen/parade_audio.py` (`py -3 -P tools/audiogen/parade_audio.py`)
| File | Length | Content |
|---|---|---|
| `assets/audio/music/mus_parade.wav` | 47.0 s one-shot | cold drum pulse (57 bpm bass drum, dry side-drum figure) over a low brass C/Db drone, tritone at the tribune, heartbeat in the crowd shot, silence under jets/Konrad, distant echo over the cleared street |
| `assets/audio/sfx/parade_boots.wav` | 6 s | boots in step on packed snow |
| `assets/audio/sfx/parade_tanks.wav` | 7 s | diesel rumble, track clatter, rattling window glass |
| `assets/audio/sfx/parade_jets.wav` | 5 s | low jet formation pass |
| `assets/audio/sfx/parade_pa_chime.wav` | 2.4 s | two-tone loudspeaker gong with plaza echo |

### Text — `data/locale/de_parade.json`

shot titles.

### Tests — `tests/visual/parade_harness.tscn` / `.gd`
`--selftest` (headless): finished once (end + skip), skip idempotent, cues complete/in order with locale text,
7–9 shots, 40–50 s, seek, banner texture follows `Symbols.enabled()`, viewport settings restored.
Windowed: frames of every shot (`--out=DIR --symbols=0|1`), exact palette check, `--realtime=47` frame cost.

<a id="part-env_plaza"></a>

## Asset manifest part — plaza-env (L_M1_Plaza art, 2026-11-05)

Walkable level "Platz vor der Großen Halle" after the parade cinematic: evening, snow, palette `berlin`, Mode B
(oblique 2.5D like Karsk), world 1920×360. All assets are original and generated deterministically by the scripts
below (licence: project-owned, no third-party material, no downloads). Level root (owned by plaza-explore):
`scenes/levels/L_M1_Plaza.tscn` with `art_manifest = res://assets/environments/hall_plaza/scene.json`.

### Generators
| Command | Writes |
|---|---|
| `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_plaza.py [--preview=DIR]` | everything under `assets/environments/hall_plaza/` incl. `scene.json`; runs the hotspot check (inside walk band, outside collisions, ≥ 26 px in the oblique metric dy×1.6) and the palette check; optional software previews |
| (module) `tools/pixelgen/env_plaza_sprites.py` | people painters (spectators from behind, guards front, marching soldiers 8-frame step), props, actor sheets, fx strips |
| `py -3 -P tools/audiogen/plaza_sfx.py` | `assets/audio/sfx/plaza_march_loop.wav` (8 s seamless loop, `smpl` chunk), `plaza_tank_pass.wav` (9 s), `plaza_shutter.wav` (0.6 s) |

Read-only reuse: `pg.py`, `env_berlin_insignia.py` (eagle / swastika masks), `env_berlin_kit.reichssiegel_mask`,
`env_karsk_lib.figure` (vendor, tank crewman; off-palette pixels snapped), `scripts/levels/karsk/karsk_actor.gd` (base).

### Layers (`scene.json` → LayerStack)
| File | Parallax / z | Content |
|---|---|---|
| `sky.png` | 0.12 / -100 | overcast dusk, cloud base lit by the searchlights, faint afterglow |
| `fx/beams.png` (12 frames, 3 fps, alpha 0.5) | 0.12 / -95 | 12 searchlight pillars ("Lichtdom"), slow sway |
| `hall.png` + `_n/_e`, `hall_hist.png` | 0.12 / -90 | Great Hall: block with pilasters and 8 banners, portico with nine piers and the state eagle, drum colonnade, ribbed patina dome, lantern with the (plain) eagle above the globe, warning lights |
| `far_plaza.png` + `_n/_e`, `far_plaza_hist.png` | 0.45 / -80 | tribune (banners, officials, lectern, floodlights), standing formations, flag masts |
| `ground.png` + `_n/_e` | 1 / -16 | avenue setts, guard strip, crowd strip, pavement with walking band and snow ridges, kerb, street with tram rails, manholes, lamp reflections, cleared lot |
| `plaza_mid.png` + `_n/_e` | 1 / -14 | guards facing the crowd, striped trestle barrier with sign board, 3 flood masts, chain line of the lot |
| `street_back.png` + `_n/_e` | 1 / -13 | side street to the archive, two tenements (sealed family door, shop shutter, balcony, lit windows, mansard, chimneys), fire wall of the cleared block |
| `fg_frame.png` | 1.25 / 60 | dark snow mounds and bollards at the bottom edge |

Decals: `props/window_life.png` (8 frames, emission), `props/notice_door.png` (+`_hist`), `props/banner_near.png`
(6 frames, +`_hist`), `props/crowd_1..7.png` (4 frames, 2 fps, playfield, base_y 266).
Props (y-sorted, collision where in the walk band): `lamp_post` ×2, `tram_stop`, `litfass` (+`_hist`), `loudspeaker`,
`children` (+`_hist`), `banner_mast`, `vendor` (emission), `photographer`, `tank_parked`, `barrel_guard` (emission),
`rubble_a`, `rubble_b`. FX strips: `fx/smoke.png`, `fx/steam.png`, `fx/exhaust.png`; snow and two fog bands.

### Actors (scenes/levels/plaza, scripts/levels/plaza)
| Scene | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| `PlazaParade.tscn` / `plaza_parade.gd` | endless procession left→right at 20 px/s, in step (7 fps): standard bearers (`actors/standard.png` + `_hist` via `Symbols.pick`), blocks of 4×9 anonymous helmeted soldiers (`actors/march_block.png`), tanks (`actors/tank.png`, exhaust puffs); z -15 between ground and guards. Drum/boots loop via `Audio.sfx_loop("plaza_march_loop", "plaza_march", dB)` following the nearest block (−9…−26 dB), stopped on exit; `plaza_tank_pass` when a tank comes on screen |
| `PlazaFlash.tscn` / `plaza_flash.gd` | the photographer's flash every 8–14 s (additive star + 0.3 s cold light, `plaza_shutter`); weak with `reduced_motion`, never a strobe |

### Contract data
Spawn `SP_Plaza_Start` (64,300). Hotspots (all inside the walk band y 270/276–352): `exit_archive`,
`exp_pz_tram_stop`, `exp_pz_window`, `exp_pz_family_door`, `exp_pz_poster`, `exp_pz_loudspeaker`, `exp_pz_children`,
`exp_pz_guard`, `exp_pz_barrier`, `exp_pz_tribune` (x 1000: hall + tribune centred on screen), `exp_pz_banner`,
`exp_pz_vendor`, `exp_pz_photographer`, `exp_pz_tank`, `exp_pz_rubble`; `hotspot_info` gives a marker point and a
description of the visible object for each. Lights: 2 street lamps, neighbours' window, vendor brazier, lot fire
barrel (warm focals), 3 cold flood masts, tribune floods.

### Insignia (§12.4) and tone
Neutral base files show the fictional Reichssiegel (black disc, white pillar) or a plain eagle; `_hist` twins show
the historical banners / wreathed eagle. The lantern figure is a plain eagle on the globe in both modes. Troops are
anonymous (helmet shadow over the eyes), the crowd is seen from behind, no salutes, no fanfare in the audio.

### Palette
All colour pixels are `berlin` palette entries plus the project's story-accent red ramp (`sepia.red`:
##4A1712 #7A2A26 #A8382E #C8503C), used only for historical banner cloth and the wax seal; binary alpha everywhere
(checked by `env_plaza.py`).

### Text
`data/locale/de_env_plaza.json`: area name, time of day, short names of the 15 spots (`env.plaza.*`).

<a id="part-explore_plaza"></a>

## Asset manifest part -- explore_plaza (exploration on the plaza before the Great Hall during the parade, Konrad)

All files below are **original works** made for this project.  Close-ups are generated procedurally by
`tools/pixelgen/explore_plaza.py`, which reuses (read-only) the close-up toolkit of `tools/pixelgen/explore_berlin.py`
(tone-space Canvas, masks, 3x5/5x7 bitmap fonts, fictional Reichssiegel, eagle and swastika bitmaps) and the
shared `pg.py` (palettes, Bayer dither, noise).  Nothing was copied, traced or sampled from
`documentation/references/` or any other source; no third-party assets, fonts or textures.  All German text was
written for this project.

* Licence / rights: project-owned original work, freely redistributable with the game.
* Provenance: deterministic generator (seeds = crc32 of `plaza.<name>`; re-running gives byte-identical files):
  `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/explore_plaza.py [--preview DIR] [names...]`.
* Date: 2026-11-05.  Size: 240 x 150 px each, pixel art 1:1, shown x3 by the DiscoveryView (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE
  12.1).  No normal/emission maps (UI images).
* Palettes: the Berlin close-up ramps of explore_berlin.py (`berlin` snow/stone/steel/violet/patina/glow, `sepia`
  greys and red, `archive` paper/ink/wood/blue/amber, `green`, `graphite`); the generator reports any colour
  outside them (none).  Light: the parade evening of `hall_plaza` (snow clouds, searchlight shafts of the
  Lichtdom, lamp-lit walls), warm accents only at the brazier, the fire barrel and the lit window.
* Insignia (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE 12.4): neutral file = fictional Reichssiegel; `*_hist.png` = real historical
  insignia, chosen at runtime by `Symbols.pick()` (`--symbols=0|1`).  Only in-world regime iconography inside the
  critical narrative (the parade poster, the facade banners, the office stamp on the eviction order); never UI,
  rewards or the player's side.  Like env's `banner_near`, the neutral banners are slate grey, `_hist` red.

| File (`assets/explore/plaza/`) | Find (`data/explore/plaza.json`) | Content |
|---|---|---|
| `tram_stop.png` | plaza.tram_stop | stop disc, closed route 74, typed notice "Sonderfahrten für Umsiedler … Güterbahnhof West" |
| `poster.png` / `poster_hist.png` | plaza.poster | parade poster on the Litfass column: dome silhouette, emblem (neutral / historical), programme, rubbed-out pencil house number |
| `vendor.png` | plaza.vendor (easter egg) | chestnut drum on a brazier, chalkboard "Kinder mit roter Nase 20 Pf", cone folded from an old city map |
| `loudspeaker.png` | plaza.loudspeaker | three-horn loudspeaker mast with snow caps, cables, crows, banner edge |
| `barrier.png` | plaza.barrier | red-white trestle barrier, chain, sign "Neuordnungsbezirk – Aufstellung nach Hausnummern", residents behind |
| `tank.png` | plaza.tank | parked tank in the cleared courtyard: snow on the hull, track, bent carpet-beating bar, crew's fire barrel |
| `tribune.png` | plaza.tribune | stepped stone tribune with slate drapes, guests in fur collars, dome in the haze, the barrier chain in front |
| `banner.png` / `banner_hist.png` | plaza.banner | 20 m banners (neutral: slate + Reichssiegel / historical: red + swastika disc), empty flat with a gathered curtain |
| `rubble.png` | plaza.rubble | fire wall with the ghost of floors, stairs and wallpapers; rubble: blue-rose wallpaper, green stove tile, nameless bell plate, blue-white "OST" cloth badge |
| `family_door.png` / `family_door_hist.png` | plaza.family_door | door of No. 9 with two laths, seal strip with red wax, enamel number, eviction order; office stamp (neutral Reichssiegel / historical eagle) |
| `window.png` | plaza.window | lit third-floor window, the neighbour drawing the curtain, a covered plate left on the sill |

Content files (same owner): `scenes/levels/L_M1_Plaza.tscn`, `data/explore/plaza.json` (13 finds + 2 chats; NPCs berlin_mother/father/child at `exp_pz_neighbour`),
`data/locale/de_explore_plaza.json`, `assets/environments/hall_plaza/explore_hotspots.json`,
`tests/test_explore_plaza.gd`.  History links (existing cards only): tribune → `hist.reich.germania`,
family_door → `hist.reich.evictions`, tram_stop → `hist.reich.reichsbahn`, rubble → `hist.reich.forced_labor`.
The fiction never quotes the real deportation sites (e.g. no "Gleis 17"): the real history stays on the cards.

## Asset manifest — plaza polish (area: plaza-polish)

Owner: plaza-polish · Date: 2026-11-05 · Engine: Godot 4.7.2 · Contract: TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.1/§12.2/§12.8
(user wish for the second Great Hall scene: "mehr infos und mehr zum erkunden", "mit aufgaben und mini games").

Everything here is **original work**, generated by deterministic project scripts (seeds = crc32 of names); no
downloads, no AI media, nothing traced. Palette: `data/palettes.json` "berlin" (+ the barrier's faded red) and the
close-up ramps of `explore_berlin.py`. **No insignia** in any of these files (no `_hist` variants needed).
Licence: original project work (same terms as the rest of the game).

### Content on the walkable parade plaza (L_M1_Plaza)

| Explore entry | Kind | Hotspot (world px) | What |
|---|---|---|---|
| `plaza.leaflet` | detail + mini-game | `exp_pz_leaflet` [856, 302], between guard and barrier | **"Das Blatt im Schnee"**: a typed carbon copy "WER FEHLT?" (names in the order of Konrad's own eviction list) drifts in the snow. Walk to it, pin it with the foot against the gusts, bend down only while both posts watch the parade. Being noticed is gentle (he straightens up, the post looks at him 1.4 s, nothing is lost); hint after two notices. Find text in Konrad's voice. |
| `plaza.frost_heart` | easter egg | `exp_pz_frost_heart` [96, 318], lamp post at the side street | A heart breathed into the hoarfrost, "H + G", below "Na endlich." |
| `plaza.paws` | easter egg | `exp_pz_paws` [1208, 312], left of the chestnut cart | A cat's tidy trail from under the barrier to the warm cart. |

All three are `Interactable` kind `discover` with `explore_id` (nodes `ExpFrostHeart`, `ExpLeaflet`, `ExpPaws`);
hotspots in `assets/environments/hall_plaza/explore_hotspots.json`, each ≥ 26 px from every other spot and inside
the walkable polygon. World cues (nodes added to `L_M1_Plaza.tscn`, z −1 under the actors): `LeafletCue`
(Sprite2D + `scripts/ui/minigames/leaflet_world_cue.gd`, hides once `plaza.leaflet` is found) and `PawTrail`.
Strings: finds in `data/locale/de_explore_plaza.json`; the mini-game's UI strings `mg.leaflet.*` in the new
`data/locale/de_minigame_plaza.json` (de_explore_plaza.json only holds entry keys, its test checks that).

### Mini-game

`scenes/ui/minigames/LeafletSnow.tscn` · `scripts/ui/minigames/leaflet_game.gd` (extends `minigame_base.gd`) +
`leaflet_logic.gd` (pure, deterministic `step(dt, dir, bend_held)`). Posts share a 16 s cycle (turn 1.1 s, sweep
3.1 s, three timing variants), calm window ≥ 5.8 s; gusts every 5.6 s from 4.2 s. Keys: ←/→ (A/D, pad) walk,
hold ↓ (S) or E/Space (pad A) to bend, Esc gives up; mouse: click the pavement to walk, hold on Konrad to bend.
Auto mode resolves after 0.2 s; `demo_solve()` uses synthetic key events only. Uses read-only: konrad and guard
`sheet.png/json`, Konrad's `portrait.png` (expression follows the situation).

### Generators and files

* `tools/pixelgen/gen_minigame_plaza.py` (`py -3 -P …`, `--preview <png>` optional) → `assets/ui/minigames/plaza/`
  at 1:1 art px, drawn ×3 nearest: `leaflet_back.png` 300×124 (sky, searchlights, colonnade, avenue, lane),
  `leaflet_march.png` 300×30 (tileable column, scrolls), `leaflet_barrier.png` 300×24, `leaflet_front.png` 300×124
  (crowd at the edges), `leaflet_bend.png` 3×40×56 (Konrad stooping, derived from `konrad/sheet.png` idle_side
  frame 0 — re-run when that sheet changes), `leaflet_sheet.png` 3×12×8, `world_leaflet.png` 2×8×6,
  `leaflet_layout.json`.
* `tools/pixelgen/explore_plaza.py leaflet frost_heart paws` → `assets/explore/plaza/{leaflet,frost_heart,paws}.png`
  (240×150 close-ups) and `assets/explore/plaza/world/paw_trail.png` (48×26 world decal).
* `tools/pixelgen/chargen_portrait2.py photographer` (spec `CAST['photographer']` in `chargen_portrait2_cast.py`:
  fedora with press card, dark gabardine coat, loosened tie, rangefinder camera on a strap) →
  `assets/characters/photographer/portrait.png` (4 expressions), `portrait_anim.png` (talk + blink rows),
  `portrait.json` — the chat speaker of `plaza.photographer` now has a face.
* Import afterwards under the shared lock (`.import_lock`).

### Verification (2026-11-05)

* `tests/test_minigames_plaza.gd` (6 cases, 129 checks): solution accepted without being noticed, posts alternate
  with a calm window, bending under a gaze is noticed and refused, bending beside the sheet finds nothing, gusts
  push / the foot pins, auto mode, keys consumed, hint after two refusals, Esc gives up, data/strings/art/portrait.
  `tests/test_explore_plaza.gd`: discover-entry bound raised from 12–14 to 12–17 (now 16).
  Full suite `--headless --script res://tests/run_all.gd`: 23 files, 178 cases, 13195 checks, all passed.
* Windowed harness (scratch, synthetic keys only): start, gaze, hint after two notices, solved via `demo_solve()`
  (2.8 s), `finished(true)`, tree unpaused after. Plaza capture `--cam=600,0` shows the leaflet cue and the paw trail.

## Asset manifest part — trans_a1m1 (cinematic LS_A1_ToM1: Alexei/Karsk → Konrad/Berlin)

* **Date:** 2026-11-04, revised 2026-11-05 (user feedback: better train animation, banners waving more with bold swastikas)
* **Area owner:** trans-a1m1 (soft protagonist switch after the A1 decision, TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.5)
* **Scene / code:** `scenes/cinematics/a1m1/CinA1ToM1.tscn`, `scripts/cinematics/a1m1/cin_a1_to_m1.gd`
  (`signal finished`, `configure(ctx)`, `play()`, `skip()`; plus `seek(t)`, `get_time()`, `get_duration()`
  for harnesses). Strings: `data/locale/de_trans_a1m1.json`. Harness: `tests/visual/cine_a1m1_harness.tscn/.gd`.
* **Generators:** `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/cine_a1m1.py` (all art + `assets/cinematics/a1m1/layout.json`);
  modules `cine_a1m1_lib.py` (ramps, dithered tone fills, 3×5 / 4×7 pixel fonts, insignia templates, savers),
  `cine_a1m1_karsk.py`, `cine_a1m1_close.py`, `cine_a1m1_berlin.py`. Sound: `py -3 -P tools/audiogen/cine_a1m1_sfx.py`.
  Built on `tools/pixelgen/pg.py` (unchanged, read-only).
* **Determinism:** every seed comes from `zlib.crc32(name)`; no `hash()`, no network, no external media.
* **Licence / provenance (all files below):** original procedural pixel art and synthesized audio created for
  ASHES OF EMPIRES: THE LAST WINTER by the trans-a1m1 generators (AI-authored code, project-owned, same licence as
  the game). No third-party images, fonts, samples or traced references.
* **Palettes:** `karsk` (dusk shot), `sepia` tone + red (ledger close-up, C-17 red), `archive` paper/blue/ink (plan),
  `berlin` (street). Grade blends sepia → archive → berlin during the dissolves (palette swapped at zero snap).
* **Re-used read-only (not copied):** `assets/environments/karsk_railyard/` `far_city`, `far_industry`, `mid`,
  `poles` layers (tinted to dusk), their smoke-ribbon strips (re-anchored from the A1 `scene.json`), props
  `wagon_c17` (the actual C-17 wagon 24-311, incl. its `_hist` variant), `lamp_post_1`, `bench`; character sheets
  `alexei`, `irina`, `konrad`, `guard`, `clerk`, `berlin_mother`, `berlin_child` (loaded at runtime).
* **Historical insignia (§12.4):** every image with real regime insignia has a neutral base file and a `_hist`
  variant, chosen from `ctx.symbols` (Game passes `Symbols.enabled()`): Soviet red star + hammer & sickle on the
  locomotive and the ledger's railway seal (neutral: fictional gear-and-wheat emblem); swastika on the Bauarchiv
  banners, the eagle relief's wreath and the GBI office stamp on the plan (neutral: the fictional Reichssiegel
  pillar). Shown only as in-world iconography of the regimes inside the critical narrative; never UI or reward.
  The Great Hall dome carries an eagle on a globe in both modes (Speer's final design had no swastika there).

Neutral: Reichssiegel on black; `_hist`: red, white disc (Ø20) with a bold 16 px 45°-turned black swastika (5-unit construction, arm width 1.2 units, hooks clockwise, exactly 4-fold symmetric). |

### Audio — `assets/audio/sfx/` (44.1 kHz, 16-bit, synthesized; not looped)
| File | Length | Content |
|---|---|---|

| `cine_a1m1_chuff.wav` | 0.8 s | One exhaust beat (played per beat, quieter when the engine is far). |
| `cine_a1m1_hiss.wav` | 2.6 s | Cylinder cocks venting. |
| `cine_a1m1_depart.wav` | 6.0 s | Low rumble of the heavy train getting under way (clanks and joints are now separate one-shots). |
| `cine_a1m1_clank.wav` | 0.8 s | One coupling snatching taut (fired at each simulated snatch, quieter down the train). |
| `cine_a1m1_clack.wav` | 0.16 s | One wheel set over a rail joint (fired per axle and joint, volume by distance to the camera). |
| `cine_a1m1_stamp.wav` | 0.9 s | Swish, wooden knock, rubber squish (layered with the existing `stamp_thud`). |
| `cine_a1m1_swell.wav` | 9.0 s | Soft open-fifth string pad under the stamp and the dissolve. |
| `cine_a1m1_tram_arrive.wav` / `cine_a1m1_tram_depart.wav` | 4.0 / 4.5 s | Tram rolling in with brake squeal / motor whine and rumble receding. |
| `cine_a1m1_door.wav` | 1.8 s | Heavy portal door: latch, swing, closing thud. |
| `cine_a1m1_pigeons.wav` | 1.4 s | Pigeons taking off. |
Existing project sounds used: `tram_bell`, `stamp_thud`, `wind_gust`, `step_snow_*`; Audio channels `amb_berlin_street`,
`mus_berlin` (continues seamlessly into M1).

### Text — `data/locale/de_trans_a1m1.json`
`cin.a1m1.alexei.share`, `cin.a1m1.alexei.keep` (inner voice, branch-dependent), `cin.a1m1.konrad` (inner voice),
`cin.a1m1.caption` (description of the match dissolve for accessibility/QA).

### Motion model (2026-11-05) -- `cin_a1_to_m1.gd`
Total length 43.0 s (`EXT` = 2.4 s: shot 1B 5.2-11.4, 1C 11.4-18.4; everything after shifted). The departure is
simulated once at build (240 Hz) and sampled, so every frame stays a pure function of t (seek/skip): regulator
opening, traction `A0` with constant power above `V_POWER`, rolling resistance; the slack of each coupling
(`SLACK` 1/2/2/2 px, buffers compressed after backing up) runs out in turn -- each snatch shares momentum and
recoils in the draft gear; one wheel slip (`SLIP`, +1.5 driver revolutions) when the whole train hangs on the
drawbar. Travel at 5.2 / 11 s after the start: 81 / 425 px (C-17 leaves the frame just before Alexei turns).
Vehicles are snapped to the 1/k viewport grid; the driver frame comes from the drawn travel (+ slip); rail joints
every 150 px give a clack per axle and a one-pixel dip; the engine heaves a pixel on each exhaust beat at low
speed; firebox and ash-pan lights pulse with the beats. Harness `--cine-mode=motion` checks rod phase vs travel,
grid, gaps/slack, no roll-back and 4 beats per revolution, and writes running-gear contact sheets.

<a id="part-trans_finale"></a>

## Asset manifest — TRANS-FINALE (X1 finale montage "Zwei Züge, ein Eintrag")

| | |
|---|---|
| Area owner | trans-finale (scenes/cinematics/finale/**, scripts/cinematics/finale/**, assets/cinematics/finale/**, tools/pixelgen/cine_finale*.py, tools/audiogen/cine_finale_sfx.py → assets/audio/sfx/cine_finale_*.wav, data/locale/de_trans_finale.json, tests/visual/cine_finale_*) |
| Date | 2026-11-04 |
| Provenance | **100 % original work, generated by code** (Python 3 + numpy + Pillow; audio with the project's own synthesis toolkit). No downloads, no reference tracing, no AI media, no samples. |
| Licence | Project-owned original work (same terms as the game's own code/art). No third-party rights. |
| Determinism | All seeds from `zlib.crc32(name)`; re-running the generators reproduces identical files. |
| Reproduce | `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/cine_finale_build.py` (≈ 60 s, all art + `finale.json`) · `py -3 -P tools/audiogen/cine_finale_sfx.py` (≈ 17 s, all sound) · then `godot --headless --path . --import` (holding `.import_lock`). Audio `.import` files use `compress/mode=0` (lossless PCM, project convention). |

### What it is
A 47.5 s animated, skippable montage that replaces the three crossfaded X1 stills. It ties the two protagonists
together softly: the same night minute (Berlin 23:47 / Karsk 1:47 Moscow time), mirrored framings (Konrad's window
faces east, Alexei's west — they "look at each other" across the cuts), the same snow carrying the cut from one
city to the other, a clock tick answered by telegraph clicks, two lamps over one border map, the red C-17 route
drawn from Karsk west to Berlin, and both lamps going out until only the line is left.

**Rework 06.11. (cinematics quality pass, user feedback „Arme komisch, Sequenzen leer“).** Same beats, shot
order, framings and subtitles; what changed:
* **Close-ups over the shoulder** (`cine_finale_figure.py`): Konrad (bareheaded: brown hair, ears, cream wool
  scarf, navy overcoat) and Alexei (ushanka, grey knit collar, green quilted jacket) sit in the foreground at the
  bottom of the frame; every arm is one chain shoulder → upper arm → elbow → forearm → wrist → hand, solved per
  frame by a two-bone IK whose screen bone lengths follow the reach (foreshortening), elbows outward or — when a
  hand crosses the body — dropped in front of the chest; the upper body leans in for far reaches. Sleeves are
  tapered tubes with elbow creases, folds, quilting (Alexei), shirt / knit cuffs; hands have a narrowing wrist,
  knuckles, four three-joint fingers with nails and creases, a two-joint thumb, skin ramps sampled from the
  portraits, and cast a dithered shadow on the desk. Irina's arm reaches in from the open door (shoulder off
  frame). Staging: the evidence branch is done by the left hand (fold, packet into the slit) while the right
  holds up the soft lined coat panel; the request branch pulls the sheet only as far as it comes free and the
  hand returns to the desk; keep_private uses a right pedestal drawer with the camera tilting down to it.
* **Desks** (`cine_finale_props.py`): Berlin — Konrad's grey felt hat, a coffee cup with steam, rolled plans, a
  pencil cup; Karsk — a green blotter under the ledger, an abacus, inkwell and dip pen, stamp pad and stamp, a
  bakelite telephone, a lit signal lantern (the ledger stamp moved clear of Alexei's head).
* **Wide shots** (`cine_finale_wide.py`): Berlin — cornice, herringbone parquet and rug, a second drawing, wall
  calendar, the dome model in a glass case, basket of rolled plans, waste-paper basket; animated pendulum (its
  extremes on the tick/tock), searchlights sweeping behind the dome, the tram's blue spark at the tram bell,
  cigarette smoke on the sill. Karsk — illuminated track diagram with blinking lamps, binders shelf, clock by the
  stove pipe, blanket and mug, coal scuttle and firewood, hanging lantern, ceiling lamp; animated freight train
  in the yard (loco mid-window on the far horn), stove firelight, kettle steam.
* **Window inserts**: searchlight sweep (Berlin); a signalman's lantern and a shunting engine's headlamp (Karsk).
* **Map** (`cine_finale_mapdetail.py`): forests, lakes, railway network with stations, roads, grid letters /
  numbers, legend, scale bar, north arrow, a pencil circle round Veltrava, kilometre ticks, a coffee ring and a
  tea-glass ring, torn corner and tape, Konrad's pencil and eraser crumbs / Alexei's pencil stub in the two lamp
  pools, the window light across the dark map; the camera drifts with the pen from Karsk to Berlin.
* Runtime: `pos_lin` (constant-speed `pos_keys`) in `finale_montage.gd`; harness `--fin-mode=endcard` renders
  the end card (title + seven decisions in story order) for any decision combination: 8 lines, no wrap, bottom
  of the caption at 840 px for the longest and the shortest texts.

**Second pass 06.11. (art-director review of the rework).** Same beats, shot order and subtitles; what changed:
* **3D arm rig** (`cine_finale_figure.py`, rewritten): arms are real 3D chains (upper arm 29 cm, forearm 26 cm,
  hand with palm, three-joint fingers and a two-joint thumb) solved by a 3D two-bone IK (elbow pole down and out,
  or in front of the chest) and projected through a perspective camera fitted to the sets (`Cam3`: 1.7 m above,
  0.4 m behind the shoulders) — the foreshortening comes from the camera, so a reaching forearm gets shorter, a
  hanging upper arm is short, hands on the desk are seen from above with the curled fingers dropping out of
  sight; beyond 86 % of the reach the upper body leans in (rotation about the hips) instead of the arm
  stretching. The old screen-space bone table (forearms growing to 172 px) is gone. Hands are z-buffered and
  smaller (in proportion to the heads); relaxed hands curl and turn inwards, no open palm points up-screen.
  The upper arms are drawn in the body layer under the back, so each sleeve runs unbroken from the shoulder
  seam over the elbow to the cuff (no elbow discs); one lamp model (set pool + 3D direction) lights both.
* **Request**: the carriage-return lever now reaches forward-left to the typist (paddle tip); the right hand
  takes the finished sheet by its corner (the camera tilts up with it), draws it towards him and lays it into
  an AUSGANG tray at the front right (the old back-left tray is now EINGANG).
* **Evidence**: the coat panel is cloth rendered per frame (12 fps): pulled up over the edge of the desk from
  under his left arm, wine-red striped satin lining with sheen and folds from the hinge, navy facing with two
  buttonholes, tailor's label, inner pocket welt with its slit; the packet is pushed into the slit (cut along
  the slit line) by the left hand, patted flat, the panel falls back. One hand does it; the right one rests.
* **Keep**: the ledger closes with a continuous page-block turn (7 frames); the pedestal drawer (widened to fit
  the book) is pulled wide open with the camera tilting down to it; the book slides across the blotter, over
  the desk edge and drops into the drawer (cut at the desk edge once inside), then rides into the desk as the
  drawer is pushed shut; sounds at the slide, the drop, the drawer and the key. Alexei's idle hand goes to his lap.
* **Share**: Irina leans in from the door (shoulder just off frame, coat shoulder at the edge): a full arm
  with upper arm, elbow and a tapering sleeve lays the note; it enters and leaves with the forearm in frame.
* **Split**: each half is framed on its sitter (`shift` per shot in `finale_montage.gd`: the set moves inside
  its fixed clip), both heads clear of the divider for all four decision combinations.
* **b_wide** continuity: Konrad now sits, bareheaded, at the same typewriter desk under the framed rendering
  (architect's lamp at its left end, his hat lying on it, the sheet in the typewriter), back to us — the close-up
  pushes in over his shoulder; he breathes, looks out at the dome and turns back to the machine.

| t (s) | shot | content |
|---|---|---|
| 0.0 | `b_wide` | Konrad seated at his typewriter desk under the framed rendering, back to us (bareheaded, hat on the desk), the floodlit Great Hall in the east window, pendulum clock; caption "Berlin, 23:47 Uhr" |
| 4.6 | `b_close` | branch `m1_choose`: **request_explanation** — he types a request on office letterhead (red ribbon for "C-17"), returns the carriage, stops, hesitates, pulls the sheet and lays it in the AUSGANG tray · **preserve_evidence** — he slides his tracing-paper copy off the stamped original, folds it twice, pulls his coat's front panel up over the edge of the desk and slips it through the slit of the inner pocket |
| 12.6 | `b_window` → `veil` → `k_window` | snow against the glass and the dome; the snow thickens into a dithered veil and becomes the snow over the Karsk yard; caption "Karsk, 1:47 Uhr Moskauer Zeit – dieselbe Minute" |
| 16.2 | `k_wide` | the signal box: Alexei (runtime sprite `assets/characters/alexei`, facing west) at his desk, lever frame, block instruments, stove |
| 20.4 | `k_close` | branch `a1_choose`: **share_with_irina** — a knock, the door opens (cold light wedge, the tea steam bends in the draught), Irina leans in from the door and lays a note on the ledger, he covers it with his hand · **keep_private** — he finishes the entry, closes the ledger, pulls the drawer wide open, slides the book over the desk edge into it, pushes it shut, locks it and pockets the key |
| 28.4 | rhyme | Berlin desk / Karsk desk (each hand lifts a finger on the tick / the telegraph click), Berlin window / Karsk window, then both desks side by side (split at the seam that becomes the line on the map) |
| 33.6 | `map` | both desk lamps (same screen positions as in the close-ups) over one schematic map; the route draws itself Karsk → Veltrava → Berlin ("Zwei fremde Orte. Dieselbe Kennung."); the Karsk lamp clicks off, then the Berlin lamp ("Ein Frieden, dessen Akten nicht übereinstimmen."); the red line fades last |

### Insignia (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.4)
In-world iconography of the regimes on official documents only, inside the critical narrative; never UI or reward.
Every insignia layer exists twice with identical frame rects; the montage picks the variant from `ctx.symbols`.

| Layer | neutral file | `_hist` file |
|---|---|---|
| Berlin office stamp on the approved plan (violet ink) | `b_close/stamp.png` — state eagle on a wreath with the fictional Reichssiegel pillar | `b_close/stamp_hist.png` — the same eagle, Hakenkreuz on point in the wreath |
| Letterhead on the typed request (paper in the machine / carried / in the tray) | `b_close/paper.png`, `b_close/letter.png` (pillar) | `b_close/paper_hist.png`, `b_close/letter_hist.png` (Hakenkreuz) |
| Ministry of Railways plate on the signal-box wall | `k_close/plaque.png` — star over the fictional gear-and-wheat | `k_close/plaque_hist.png` — star over hammer & sickle, "МПС" |
| Round stamp in Alexei's freight ledger (red ink) | `k_close/stamp.png` (gear-and-wheat) | `k_close/stamp_hist.png` (star, hammer & sickle) |

The small symbols are hand-pixelled masks (7×7 / 9×9, `cine_finale_kit.SYM`), the eagle is rasterised from a
polygon description (`eagle_shapes`); all original drawings.

### Runtime
`scenes/cinematics/finale/Finale.tscn` (root script `scripts/cinematics/finale/finale_montage.gd`, snow node
`finale_snow.gd`): `signal finished` (exactly once), `configure(ctx)`, `play()`, `skip()` (idempotent), plus
`seek(t)` for tests. Everything visual is a pure function of the montage time. Reduced motion: static cameras, no
flicker. Grade preset `grade_preset = "finale"` (falls back to `default`).

### Verification (2026-11-04)
* `tests/visual/cine_finale_harness.tscn` (boots the real Main with `--cinematic=none`, writes no saves):
  shots of all 4 branch combinations × 24 times, both symbol modes, subtitles in the full window;
  real-time run: `finished` 1×, return value correct, frame time avg 4.2 ms / p95 4.2 ms (one 144 ms frame at
  instancing, behind the black fade); skip at 12.5 s with a second skip press: `finished` 1×, skipped = true,
  montage freed, camera released.
* `tests/visual/cine_finale_symcheck.gd` (headless): every neutral/_hist pair exists, imports and differs.

## Asset manifest part — Chapter 3 „Der eiserne Frieden" (opening), branch `kapitel-3`

Date for all entries: **2026-11-05** (work started 2026-11-04). Everything below is original work for this project, produced
by deterministic generators in this repository or written by hand; no downloaded images, sounds or samples, no AI image
services, no tracing of references. Licence: project-owned, same as the game. The real-history cards cite published
books and encyclopaedia articles as sources (text written here, not copied); their facts could not be checked online in
the cloud session and carry a `review` note in the data.

`tests/test_c3_puzzle_ui.gd`, `tests/visual/ProtocolPuzzlePreview.tscn`).

## Asset manifest — Bahnhof Veltrava, polish pass (area: env_veltrava_station)

Owner: env_veltrava_station · Date: 2026-11-06 · Engine: Godot 4.7.2 (Forward+, checked on the PC with an RTX 4090)
· Contract: TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §3.1, §12.2 (actors), §12.4 (insignia), C3_DESIGN §4.1, C3_REPORT §4.2/§4.6.

Everything here is **original work**, generated by the deterministic project scripts listed below (fixed seeds, no
network); no downloads, no AI media, no tracing — the second, independent station version on the local branch
`merge-kapitel-3` (worktree `C:/Spiele/AshesOfEmpires_c3`, commit `2260cf0`) was only *looked at* in the running game
and its ideas re-drawn here (glazed roof, occupied lit compartments, sandstone concourse); no file of it was copied.
Palette: `veltrava` (`data/chapters/c3.json`, registered by `tools/pixelgen/env_veltrava_lib.py`). Licence: original
project work (same terms as the rest of the game).

Rebuild: `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_veltrava_station.py` (art + scene.json + documents + document alt texts).
Two consecutive runs on the PC are **byte-identical** (115 files hashed). PNG bytes differ from the cloud build of
2026-11-05 only by the Pillow/zlib encoder (the pixels of an unchanged generator were verified identical);
JSON outputs are now written with LF on every platform.

### What changed (all in `assets/environments/veltrava_station/**`, regenerated)

| Area | Change (why) |
|---|---|
| Value structure / dawn | The cloud pass read as night on the PC: the far planes lost ~25 % to canvas modulate + grade + palette snap. Sky stops one ramp step lighter plus a rose-cream dawn band (`sky.png`), softer cloud cores (`clouds.png`), the mansard city hazed half a step (`city.png`), canvas modulate `#C9CED8` → `#D5D3D6`. Mean frame luminance now matches Karsk (73–79 vs 73), the sky is the light group, trains/canopy the dark frame. |
| Station building (0.55) | Warm sandstone in shade (cream ramp) instead of slate → separates from the cold city haze and carries the warmth of the lit hall window. |
| Concourse façade | Cream sandstone instead of a monotone dark-blue wall (was 70 % of the cam-1280 frame); shadow under the canopy eaves, warm café spill on the stone, dark wooden shopfront frames, readable incised lettering. |
| Canopy (`canopy.png`) | Ridge-and-furrow roof lights in every bay (wired glass, snow patches, glazing bars) — idea from the worktree's glass shed; first track sign moved to x 226 (it hung under the HUD objective box at cam 0). |
| Near train (`train_near.png`) | ~⅓ of the compartments lit with backlit curtains and seated passengers; car 3 frosted/misted (side task „Wagen drei ist kalt“: no heating since Kordon); the tied-back drape that read as a crescent moon replaced by side curtains + pelmet. |
| Platform (`ground.png`) | Lit from the open side (lighter rear band, darker under the roof), warm reflections of the lit compartments in the wet slabs, footprint trails that cross the drift, step over the coping and continue in the track-bed snow (find `exp_footprints`). |
| Foreground (`fg_frame.png`) | Fluted cast-iron columns with capitals, plinths, lamp rim light and a red fire-alarm box instead of plain black bars; three low near-camera silhouettes (crate barrow with mail sack, sand bin with shovel, suitcase pile). |
| Props matching their find texts | poster „DREI KORRIDORE – EIN FRIEDEN“, peeling, one pin left (`column_5`); the same photo on three front pages (`kiosk`); RM / RBL / VKR chalk rates, corrected (concourse); „Büro für Rückführung“ no longer clipped, „Zimmer 4“ (concourse); strapped delegation case No. 14 with the wooden horse on a trouser belt (`luggage_cart`); two porters warming their hands at the stove, coal scuttle (concourse). |
| Insignia (§12.4) | Neutral Reich flag/pennants are now a reading grey (like the Berlin facade banner) with the fictional Reichssiegel; `_hist` twins unchanged in design. Both settings captured in game. |
| Smoke | 4 + 2 chimney ribbons instead of 6 + 4 (they read as scratches across the old town). |
| Normal maps | Audit of all 18 props: no inverted/flat-broken maps (n.z ≥ 0.3 everywhere, cylinder normals on the columns, bevel normals elsewhere). |

the border officer, Voronova, Konrad and the baked queue / bench figures). Sounds reused: `berlin_pigeon_coo`,
`berlin_pigeon_flutter`, `tram_bell`, `cine_a1m1_tram_arrive`, `cine_a1m1_tram_depart`, `karsk_train_far`.

### Ambient life (scene.json `actors`, `decals`)
* **Pigeons** (`exp_pigeons`): two tug at the sandwich, a third waits, two more peck; a walking/standing observer is
  tolerated, running past (or stepping into them) sends them up to the canopy girder; back 16–28 s later.
* **Island walkers**: up to 6 of 7 people (travellers, delegation staff, railwayman with hand lamp, porter with barrow)
  on the island platform, pausing at two spots; spawn and leave hidden behind wagon 4 / the concourse.
* **Tram** in the square (seen through the exit arch): waits 34–46 s, leaves, the next one rolls in (bell).
* **Viaduct train**: a small goods train crosses the river viaduct every 55–75 s (alternating direction).
* **Decal hook** `heater_leak_23`: the steam leak of the frozen stop cock between wagons 2 and 3 (side task „Wagen
  drei ist kalt“), named so the task code can stop it: `level.backdrop.decals_by_name["heater_leak_23"].visible = false`.

## Asset manifest part -- env_veltrava_hall (Chapter 3, Palais der Kommission, L_C3_Hall)

All files below are **original works generated procedurally** by the scripts in `tools/pixelgen/`
(`env_veltrava_hall.py` drives `env_veltrava_hall_room.py`, `_props.py`, `_ext.py`, `_fg.py`, `_fx.py`, `_life.py`,
helpers `env_veltrava_hall_kit.py`, `env_veltrava_hall_layout.py` and the shared, read-only `pg.py`; previews
`env_veltrava_hall_preview.py`).  No pixels were copied, traced, sampled, downloaded or AI-generated; no third-party
assets, fonts or textures are embedded; the bitmap fonts are hand-defined in `env_veltrava_hall_kit.py`.

* Licence / rights: project-owned original artwork (generated for this project), freely redistributable with the
  game.  Provenance: deterministic generator (a re-run reproduces every file byte-for-byte on the same Python/Pillow
  build; the cloud build and this PC produced identical pixels, only the PNG compression bytes differ), re-run with `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_veltrava_hall.py --scene-only` (the hall documents
  in `assets/ui/documents/veltrava_hall_*` are rebuilt only without `--scene-only`; their owner decides).
  Machine-readable list: `assets/environments/veltrava_hall/provenance.json`.
* Dates: 2026-11-05 (cloud build, Compatibility renderer) · 2026-11-06 round 7 (Forward+ pass on this PC: calmer
  marble floor, open archway, entrance transom, raking stair wainscot, the *PAX 1953* allegory and the president's
  medallion, office wall structure (pipe, picture rail, delegations' pigeonholes, session indicator, Saal monitor
  speaker), tufted acoustic wall and three etched transoms in the interpreters' corridor, the agenda board,
  thresholds, the horseshoe table of the Saal readable through the booths, and the ambient life in `life/`).
* Palettes: `palais` (data/chapters/c3.json + one cold near-white) for the interior and the Saal layers, the
  Veltrava town palette for everything seen through the windows.  The life figures use the palais ramps for
  clothes and the cast's four skin tones (as in `assets/characters`) so they sit with the story characters.
* Insignia (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE 12.4): `props/flag_ussr.png`, `props/flag_reich.png` and `saal_far.png` are the
  neutral defaults (fictional gear-and-wheat emblem / fictional Reichssiegel); the `*_hist.png` twins carry the
  historical flags and are chosen at runtime by `Symbols.pick()` -- in-world iconography of the regimes inside the
  critical narrative only.  No life strip shows an insignia (the usher wears the commission's bridge badge).
* Content: the people are working staff and delegates -- nobody heroic, nobody caricatured; the allegory *PAX 1953* is
  the commission's own commissioned picture and is shown as cool and a little empty.

## Asset manifest part — c3_cine (Chapter-3 cinematics LS_C3_Trains, LS_C3_ToHall, LS_C3_Close)

* **Date:** 2026-11-05/06 (finishes the c3-cine work started in the cloud session of 2026-11-04/05)
* **Area owner:** cinematics stream (C3_DESIGN.md §2.9, §7)
* **Scenes / code:** `scenes/cinematics/ch3_trains/` (`Ch3Trains.tscn`, `ch3_trains.gd`), `ch3_tohall/`, `ch3_close/`,
  shared `ch3_common/` (`c3_cinematic.gd` = the §12.5 contract: `finished` exactly once, idempotent `skip()`,
  `configure(ctx)` picks the inner lines from the story flags, `seek()`/`advance()` for tools and tests, cue list for
  subtitles/sound/loops, reduced motion; `pixel_fx.gd` = deterministic snow and 1 px Bresenham wires/hands;
  `c3_dissolve*.gdshader` = ordered-dither dissolve, one Bayer cell per world pixel). Registered in
  `data/chapters/c3.json` → `cinematics`, played by `ChapterExt.play_cinematic()`.
* **Generators:** `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_veltrava_cine.py [--only trains,tohall,close]` writes every texture and
  `assets/cinematics/ch3/layout.json` (anchors shared with the runtimes; `tests/test_c3_cinematics.gd` checks the
  match-cut geometry, the clock centre and the platform projection against it). Modules:
  `env_veltrava_cine.py` (trains interiors + exterior + flare, build/main), `env_veltrava_cine_dawn.py` (t4),
  `env_veltrava_cine_tohall.py`, `env_veltrava_cine_close.py`, `env_veltrava_cine_kit.py` (fonts via
  `env_veltrava_lib`, rings, dial indices, dithered puffs), `env_veltrava_cine_figs.py` (the two protagonists).
  Sound: `py -3 -P tools/audiogen/c3_cine_audio.py` → `assets/audio/sfx/c3cine_*.wav`.
* **Figures:** Konrad and Alexei at the compartment windows (from behind), their faces in the black glass and
  Konrad's profile are sculpted and lit with the signed-distance pipeline of the dialogue portraits
  (`chargen_portrait2_anat/_cast/_paint/_sdf`, imported read-only, nothing there modified): the specs `konrad()` /
  `alexei()` supply heads, hats, hair and coat materials, so the cinematic figures follow the portraits. Renders are
  memoised on disk (`%TEMP%/ashes_c3cine_cache`, keyed by the pipeline sources + arguments, results byte-identical;
  `ASHES_NO_CACHE=1` disables it).
* **Figures, round 10 (figures stream):** the round-7 busts had two capsules per arm with the forearms hidden behind
  the back and a hand painted separately into the room, so arms read as stumps growing out of the chest. New modules:
  `env_veltrava_cine_rig.py` (one continuous chain per arm: shoulder, upper arm, elbow with the sleeve's folds and
  the point of the elbow, forearm, shirt cuff, wrist, a hand of palm, knuckles, three phalanges per finger and a
  two-joint thumb; limbs modelled in view space with radii tapering toward the window, which fakes the room's
  perspective; point lights — reading lamp, tea glass, chandelier — added to the painter; split finger materials
  for the dark gaps between fingers), `env_veltrava_cine_heroes.py` (seated Konrad: left forearm along the table, the
  hand holding the cup by its handle, thumb on top, the right elbow out, the buttoned club chair hiding his hips;
  seated Alexei: the right hand at the holder's handle, quilted sleeves with stitched rings, the satchel strap on
  the outer coat; breath frames lift only head and shoulders, hand and chair stay put; Konrad walking under the
  palais clock as an 8-frame cycle; the contact shadows of the arms on the tables) and
  `env_veltrava_cine_people.py` (full-body extras from the same rig — legs with knees and shoes, coats that open
  with the stride, 17 kinds from travellers to the police of the Free City, walk / stand / sit poses, dawn / day /
  palais / night light presets; `person_sheet()` is the API the places builder calls; the passengers behind the lit
  windows of the dawn trains, Konrad and Alexei at one window each; the two hotel-window silhouettes as hand-set
  pixel templates). `env_veltrava_cine_figs.py` is unchanged (the other film families import it).
* **Figures, round 10b (art-director pass):** the hands at the cup and the tea glass are drawn at the scale of
  the cup and the glass (~1.85 px/cm; round 10's were 14 x 11 and 18 x 16 px mittens): hook grips with the first
  phalanges straight out of the knuckles (`rig.hand(joints=...)`), the thumb on top, and `finger_marks` drawing
  the gaps between the four fingers along their projected axes (`rig.hand_axes`); Alexei's fist closes round the
  holder's handle, whose bar the sprite repeats in lit nickel silver. Konrad's hat: a real ribbon band
  (`hat_band`) and a crown that ends at the brim (`fedora_cut`; the portrait crown's felt strip under the brim made
  the dashed line round the crown). The walking Konrad is a whole man (see `k2_konrad`), his rims drawn along the
  outline (`contour_rim`). The extras got profile faces (`face_marks`: eye, brow, closed mouth, ear; heads carried
  forward), straight-hanging men's coats, and cases / lanterns hanging from the fist by their handles.
* **Determinism:** fixed seeds everywhere (`np.random.default_rng(n)`, `zlib.crc32(name)` for audio); two full builds
  are byte-identical (checked with md5 over all PNG/WAV outputs).
* **Licence / provenance (all files below):** original procedural pixel art and synthesized audio made for ASHES OF
  EMPIRES: THE LAST WINTER by these generators (AI-authored code, project-owned, same licence as the game). No
  downloaded, copied or traced images, fonts or samples. No real insignia anywhere in these cinematics (pennants and
  flags are plain), so there are no `_hist` variants.
* **Palettes:** `veltrava` and `palais` ramps of `data/chapters/c3.json` (registered by `env_veltrava_lib`) plus the
  protagonists' material ramps of the portrait pipeline; grade `c3_cine` (no palette snap, light vignette and grain).

| `pass_river`, `pass_river_obj` (4 cells) | (AD round 2 rework) the frozen river seen from the truss bridge in perspective: a ground image painted with the river's centre under the glass (scoured dark ice, snow tongues running down the river, an open lead with sea smoke and frost on its rims, white fracture lines, a trodden path and a sled track across, fishing holes, the snowy banks with reeds frozen in, the bend where the river turns away between two clumps of pollard willows and a fisherman's hut with a lit window). The runtime scrolls every row at the speed of its own depth (`layout.json` `trains.river`: vanishing row, bend, bottom-row speed), so the valley swings past like a wiper and slides in from the window's edge; the lateral ends fade into the plain. `pass_river_obj` = upright things on the ice drawn at the depth of their foot: an ice-fishing tent with its lantern, a fisher at the hole (figures stream), a rowing boat frozen in by the bank, a marker pole. The bridge is world-locked (portals pass the glass at 11.7 / 13.7 s, 13 bays, inclined end posts); over it the embankment strip and the car's window light on the snow give way to the drop |

| `int_konrad`, `fig_konrad` (4 frames), `refl_konrad`, `glow_lamp`, `patch_warm` | the German delegation's saloon car (figures: `env_veltrava_cine_heroes`, figures stream); the runtime adds the coffee's steam and the curtain tassels swinging with the joints |
| `int_alexei`, `fig_alexei` (4 frames), `refl_alexei`, `glow_tea`, `steam_tea` (6), `spoon` (2), `patch_cold` | the Soviet compartment (figures: `env_veltrava_cine_heroes`); the runtime adds the towel on the berth rail swinging with the joints |
| `sweep`, `flare`, `roomflare_k`, `roomflare_a` | the floodlit level crossing of the match cut (light band, the glass flooded, the room and the sitter's rim lit); `roomflare_*` also carries the flash of the passing lamps; the runtime adds the crossing's blinking red lamps and cross sign, two green signals |
| `l3_bg`, `l3_fg`, `l3_glow` | (round 10, `env_veltrava_cine_line.py`, t3b) the Line before dawn from high above in one-point perspective: night sky with the low cloud lit by the fence and by the city, the eastern horizon paling, the Free City glowing at the vanishing point; the snow plain, the raked control strip and patrol road between the double fence, light pools of the masts, both tracks on their embankments with telegraph poles and wires, villages with a steeple and an onion dome; `l3_fg` = fence posts, floodlight masts and watchtowers in front of the trains; `l3_glow` = mast halos and the city glow. The runtime draws both trains in the same projection (`line.proj`): lit windows on the inner sides, Konrad's salon car, Alexei's sealed wagon 4, red tail lamps, the plumes lit by the fireboxes, the towers' searchlights, snow |
| `d4_sky`, `d4_sun_glow` | (round 10, `env_veltrava_cine_city.py`, t4 crane plane „sky", travel 40 px) winter dawn: slate overhead, rose-cream low in the east, stratus banks with lit undersides, the low sun (it climbs out of the station roof while the camera rises) and its additive warmth |
| `d4_city`, `d4_dome_glow` | (plane „city", travel 105 px) Veltrava from the river: the castle hill (AD round 2: a crag of rock buttresses with snow on the ledges, spruce stands and birches on the flanks, the road zig-zagging up between retaining walls and lamps to the gatehouse; the castle with curtain wall, merlons and arrow slits, two round towers with conical roofs, the keep with its corbelled timber gallery and steep roof under a plain pennant, the palas with dormers, chimneys and lit windows, a chapel with its ridge turret, the stair tower's copper cap -- dawn on the east faces), the cathedral with two spires, the palais of the Commission (portico, pilasters, drum and the patinated dome catching the first sun), the onion church, the town hall with its copper spire and lit clock, the station's arched glass hall and tower, the old town (gables, mansards, stepped gables, brick and stucco, snow on every roof, lit windows), the quay rank with shopfronts, tram rails, wire poles and lamps, the quay wall and the water beyond the bridge with floating ice; `d4_dome_glow` = first sun on the dome and the station roof |
| `d4_bridge`, `d4_parapet`, `d4_lamp_glow` | (plane „bridge", travel 140 px) the three-arch stone bridge (far railing, deck, far track, signal posts, spandrel, voussoirs, icicles; the arches open so the far quay shows through with parallax), the river in front (dark water, short dawn streaks, the arches mirrored, ice sheets with pressure ridges and a wet rim, floes, a fishing hole and its wind screen, a sled track); the near parapet with its seven lanterns (in front of the trains) and their halos |

Legs by two-bone IK on a gait table (heel strike, flat foot, heel off, toe off, the swing with the knee folding; the planted foot slides back exactly one stride per cycle), the coat opening over the forward thigh, the arms swinging against the legs; lit by the chandelier with a continuous cold rim on his back and a warm rim along the hat and shoulders (drawn along the outline), a soft contact shadow on the marble under his feet. `layout.json` `tohall.konrad_path` (soles, he walks from the left edge at the cut to under the clock at 6 s), `konrad_anchor`, `konrad_stride` (88 px per cycle = 1.1 s, phased on the footstep sounds), `konrad_frames` |
| `ex_k1_*`, `ex_k2_*` (round 10, figures stream) | the extras (`env_veltrava_cine_people.EXTRAS['tohall']`, drawn by `scenes/cinematics/ch3_common/c3_people.gd` from `layout.json` `tohall.extras`): under the station clock a porter, a woman with her suitcase, the station master with his signal disc, a policeman of the Free City and a waiting man on the far platform; in the palais an usher at the door to the hall, two delegates talking by the wainscot, a secretary hurrying ahead of Konrad toward the hall, her briefcase hanging from her hand by its handle. Round 10b: every extra has a profile face (eye, brow, closed mouth, the ear; the head carried forward, never tipped back) and cases and lanterns hang from the fist |
Hands are drawn at runtime (1 px Bresenham) from the shown time 9:39:57 → 9:40:00.

| `n2_bg`, `n2_win_konrad` (6), `n2_win_alexei` (6), `n2_lamp_glow` | the station square at night: Hotel Europa (mansard, balconies) and Hotel Nord (flat roof), snow on every ledge, the street into the old town with the palais dome, the lime trees and a parked car with someone smoking in it (runtime ember); the two lit windows where Konrad and Alexei step to the glass and turn toward the whistle. Round 10: more lit rooms in both hotels, lamps down the street into the old town, the kiosk with its shutters down, an advertising column, a telephone box, the tram stop, a taxi waiting at the Europa (runtime exhaust), the lobbies' light spilling onto the snow, footprint trails, bollards with their chain; the runtime raises a plume of steam over the roofs as the one whistle sounds. On platform 5 the goods train got real wagons (box cars with planks, straps and doors, tank wagons, a tarpaulin load; the end facing us with its brake wheel and tail board), a luggage trolley with mail sacks, crates, footprints; the engine's steam is soft drifting cloud. AD round 2: the right third is no longer an empty field under a straight horizon -- platform 6 under its own canopy (every other lamp lit, pools on the trodden snow, columns, the sign „6", a bench, a mail barrow), a sleeping-car train standing on track 8 with two lit compartments, the goods shed's snowy roof, the water tower and a floodlight mast of the yard over the goods train, a signal gantry, a water crane with its fire pot glowing, a plank crossing over tracks 5 and 6; the luggage barrow drawn as an oak barrow with canvas mail sacks tied at the neck, a parcel in string, a wicker hamper, the drawbar lowered; wooden crates instead of dark boxes |
| `ex_n1_*`, `ex_n2_*` (round 10, figures stream) | the extras of `EXTRAS['close']` (`layout.json` `close.extras`, placed in the platform's pinhole projection): a railwayman with his lantern at the platform edge, two men in long coats watching the special go, a policeman of the Free City by the near pillar; on the square a policeman walking his beat under the lamps, the Europa's doorman, a late traveller crossing from the station. The window silhouettes (`n2_win_*`) are hand-set pixel templates: head and jaw, neck, sloping shoulders, the turned profile with nose and chin, Alexei's tea glass at his chest, the room lamp's rim. Round 10b: six states shown on `close.win_seq` (each man comes in at the back of the room as a dim figure under the ceiling lamp, steps to the glass past the curtain, stands at the glass, turns his head halfway and then into profile after the whistle) instead of popping from an empty window to a silhouette |

### Checks
`tests/test_c3_cinematics.gd` (17 cases; round 10: 36 s Trains with shots t1 t2 t3 t3b t4, the layout keys of the
journey / Line / crane planes, both dissolves, the crane's plane offsets and its reduced-motion hold): registration, contract (`finished` once for skip/skip, skip before play,
natural end then skip, coarse fast-forward, replay), line variants for every flag combination, subtitles spoken in
order and localised, no cues after skip, reduced motion (no knocks, no roll), shot order, every texture and every cue
sound present, layout sync (glass/exterior box, clock centre, projection, runtime anchors), rail joints of the picture
== joints cut into the bed sounds, the dawn dissolve completes. Visual review: `tests/visual/c3cine_harness.tscn`
(`--c3-mode=shots|seq|run`).
`tests/test_c3_figures.gd` (5 cases, round 10 figures stream): the seated sheets have 4 frames and boxes that reach
the cup / the holder's handle, the palais walk has 8 frames, every extra has its sheet, an anchor inside the frame
and a time-ordered path, `c3_people.gd` interpolates paths and steps walk cycles by the distance walked, ToHall and
Close mount one people drawer per shot and follow the timeline.

## Asset manifest — Chapter-3 mini-games (area: minigames r7, Veltrava)

Owner: minigames r7 (Veltrava) · Date: 2026-11-06 · Engine: Godot 4.7.2 · Contract: TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.8
(user request this round: more play time, new mini-game ideas, finish Chapter 3).

Everything listed here is **original work**, generated procedurally by project scripts. No pixel, glyph, sample or
melody was taken from `documentation/references/`, a font file or any external source; no downloads, no AI media.
The three bitmap fonts (7×11 flap capitals, 5×7 sign letters incl. the Cyrillic of the station signs, 3×5 plate
digits) are hand-made in `tools/pixelgen/mg_r7_v_kit.py`. Colours: `data/chapters/c3.json` palettes `veltrava`
(station) and `palais` (palace) plus documented mixes of two palette entries (skin tones, camel, charcoal); the
greens of scarves and the Soviet delegation pin use `veltrava.patina`. **No state insignia anywhere**: the
"delegation pins" are a fictional conference system told apart by shape (square Reich delegation, triangle Soviet
delegation, diamond observers, circle commission). The maker's plate "W. HOLUB · VELTRAVA" is fictional; no brands.
Licence: original project work (same terms as the rest of the game).

### The two mini-games

| Explore entry | Scene / scripts | Place (hotspot, world px) | Game |
|---|---|---|---|
| `veltrava.station.flap_board` (detail, Alexei) | `scenes/ui/minigames/FlapBoard.tscn` · `flap_game.gd` + `flap_logic.gd` | `L_C3_Station` `exp_flap_board` [548, 304] below canopy column 3; world cue `FlapBoardCue` (`flap_world_cue.gd`) | The arrivals board ANKUNFT · ПРИБЫТИЕ · ARRIVÉE hangs in its top row at Alexei's own train. Six flap drums (hour 24, tens 6, units 10, from 10, via 8, platform 8 flaps) only fall forward; four couplings (units→tens carry on 9→0, tens→hour, from→via, track→via) make the order matter. Target = the printed timetable's special train **8:41 MOSKVA über KARSK, Gleis 5**. The line engages by itself; „Probelauf“ names what differs. The KARSK card is new (white, no yellowing) — Alexei's detail. |
| `veltrava.hall.cloak_tokens` (detail, Konrad) | `scenes/ui/minigames/Cloakroom.tscn` · `cloak_game.gd` + `cloak_logic.gd` | `L_C3_Hall` `exp_cloak_tokens` [205, 300], beside the text agent's `veltrava.hall.cloakroom` (240); world cue `WenzelCue` (`cloak_world_cue.gd`) behind the counter | Session break; Herr Wenzel (garderobier since 1927) hung the coats on the wrong hooks. Round 1: four guests (Dr. Imhof, Frau Orlova, M. Duval, Herr Bauer), five coats; round 2: three of them come back for their hats, wearing their coats; six hats. Clues: pin shape, newspaper (Berlin / Moscow / Zurich), scarf colour, dictionary; the hook numbers mislead. Elena Weiss's charcoal coat with the wine-red scarf (check 27) stays — consistent with the cloakroom find and Frau Brunner's chat. |
