<a id="part-minigames_r7_relay"></a>

## Asset manifest — Chapter-3 mini-game „Das Relaispult“ (area: minigames_r7_relay)

Owner: minigames_r7_relay specialist · Date: 2026-11-06 · Engine: Godot 4.7.2 · Contract: TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.1/§12.8
(user request this round: more play time, new mini-games, finish Chapter 3).

Everything listed here is **original work**, generated procedurally by the two project scripts below on 2026-11-06.
No pixel, sample, voice or melody was taken from `documentation/references/` or any other source; no downloads, no
AI media, no recordings. Colours come from `data/chapters/c3.json` (palettes `palais` and `veltrava`) plus three
lamp-glass ramps defined in the generator. No flags, no insignia, no text baked into the art (all text is Loc).
The voices are prosody only (glottal source, vowel formants, consonant noise) — they speak no language and no words.
Historical model: the booth-and-lamp systems of the post-war conferences (the real-history card
`hist.reich.nuremberg_interpreters` tells the Nuremberg story outside the fiction; in the game world the trial never
happened, so nothing in-world refers to it). Licence: original project work (same terms as the rest of the game).

### The mini-game

| Explore entry | Scene / scripts | Place | Game |
|---|---|---|---|
| `veltrava.hall.relay` (detail, area `veltrava`, links `hist.reich.nuremberg_interpreters`) | `scenes/ui/minigames/RelayDesk.tscn` · `scripts/ui/minigames/relay_game.gd` + `relay_logic.gd`; hotspot `scripts/ui/minigames/relay_spot.gd`; world prop `relay_world_desk.gd` | L_C3_Hall, interpreters' corridor, `exp_relay_desk` [1777, 300] between Kabine 3 and the fire box; prop `RelayDeskProp` (feet [1777, 295]) | The commission's sound technician has to go down to the Saal; Konrad keeps the interpretation desk. Four delegation channels (DE Reichsdelegation, RU Sowjetische Delegation, FR Vorsitz der Kommission, EN Beobachter) are plugged into the floor original or one of three booths (Kabine 1 · FR with German retour, Kabine 2 · RU↔DE, Kabine 3 · EN); booths that do not understand the floor take the relay of another booth and fall behind sooner. Yellow lamp: the speaker slows down; red: he stops a moment. Three rounds (Winterfahrplan · Lebensmittelzüge · Geschäftsordnung) with rising tempo and shorter warnings; a passage is repeated when less than 68 % got across or four threads were lost. The chair adjourns; the interpreter of Kabine 1 has the last word. Open after `c3b_arrival` until `c3b_inspect_booth` (the session) and in a memory of the palais (§12.10). |

Contract (§12.8, shared base `minigame_base.gd`): `open(ctx)`, `ctx.auto` resolves at once, `finished(success)` exactly
once, hint after two refusals (contextual: lamps after lost threads, guide lights on the right sockets after a wrong
desk, the announcements as third hint; Settings `puzzle_hints` 0 off / 2 at once), Esc gives up without penalty,
„Neu anfangen“ repeats the current round, `demo_solve()` plays the whole session through synthetic key events only
(~140 s). Keys: ↑/↓ channel, ←/→ move its plug, 1–4 plug straight in, G (pad X) yellow, R (pad Y) red,
Space/Enter/E (pad A) start/continue; mouse on sockets, tags, lamps and the display. Every state reads by text and
shape as well as colour; desk labels follow the text-scale setting as far as their plates allow (a too long line
shrinks instead of overflowing; the flavour subtitle gives way at 125/150 %); high contrast brightens needles,
selection, guides and the dim texts; reduced motion stops all blinking. All text: `data/locale/de_minigames_r7_relay.json`.

### Generators

* `tools/pixelgen/gen_mg_relay.py` — `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_mg_relay.py`, then import (shared lock).
  Deterministic (seeds = `crc32` of names; a re-run reproduces every file byte for byte). Drawn 1:1; the game draws
  the desk x3 (nearest), the DiscoveryView shows the close-up x3, the prop lives at world scale.
* `tools/audiogen/mg_audio_relay.py` — `py -3 -P tools/audiogen/mg_audio_relay.py` (numpy only, deterministic).
  44.1 kHz / 16-bit mono WAV in `assets/audio/sfx/`; loops are built periodic and carry a RIFF `smpl` chunk.

### Files

### Verification (2026-11-06)

* `tests/test_minigames_r7_relay.gd` (10 cases, ~1700 checks): booth/relay/pivot tables for every floor language,
  rising tempo and shorter warnings, a careful and a slower scripted player get through (no lost thread, ≥ 90 %),
  never re-plugging repeats round 1, ignoring the lamps repeats round 2 (lost threads), unneeded lamps are
  ignored, yellow slows / red pauses (no scoring) / a lost thread silences the relaying booths, auto mode, keys,
  hint, Esc, demo_solve source check, find + strings (calm, typographic quotes, no microphone numbers, nothing about
  Anlage C or Elena), hotspot on the walkable floor ≥ 26 px from every other spot, level nodes, art sizes vs layout,
  WAV format and loop chunks.
* Windowed captures (scratch harnesses, not in the repo): the desk over the palais at 100 %, 150 % text and high
  contrast; the real game booted into L_C3_Hall, Konrad at the desk, opened through `Game.interact` →
  `_run_minigame`, solved by `demo_solve()` in 140 s without a refusal, find + history card shown, game unpaused.

Peak −5 dBFS, RMS −26 dBFS. No chime, no fanfare. |
| `assets/audio/sfx/mem_return.wav` | 2.6 s, same format | same | The same clock forward, fast at first, settling into tick-tock; the swell from its peak fading out; drone rising back. Peak −5 dBFS, RMS −31 dBFS. |

Determinism: `gen_ui_memories.py` and `memories_audio.py` are seeded (zlib.crc32 of the names) and write byte-identical
files on every run (checked twice: md5 of both WAVs and all PNGs unchanged). The raw captures are renderings of the
running game (snow, walkers, smoke move), so a re-capture gives slightly different frames; the print step from a given
raw frame is deterministic.

Reproduce:
```bash
GODOT="$LOCALAPPDATA/Programs/Godot/Godot_v4.7.2-stable_win64_console.exe"
"$GODOT" --path . res://tests/visual/memory_thumbs.tscn -- --scene=L_A1_Railyard --capture=C:/tmp/boot.png \
    --frames=100000 --mt-out=C:/Spiele/AshesOfEmpires/assets/ui/memories/src --symbols=0
py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_ui_memories.py
py -3 -P tools/audiogen/memories_audio.py
## then the shared --import (lock .import_lock)
```
`--mt-survey=1` captures a 320-px camera sweep per level (to choose the spots in `SHOTS` / `CROPS`).

### 2. Code / data
* `scripts/core/memories.gd` (helper), `scripts/core/game.gd` (memory mode, transitions, films), `scripts/core/memory_drive.gd`
  (E2E helpers + `--memory-demo`), `scripts/ui/memory_view.gd` + `scenes/ui/MemoryView.tscn`, HUD badge (`hud.gd`),
  pause / main menu entries, save guard (`save_system.gd`, `save_load_menu.gd`).
* `data/grades.json` → `"memory"` overlay preset. Strings: `data/locale/de_memories.json` (UI, places, months, prompts,
  remembered lines, film titles, `sys.save.err.memory`).
* Profile file at runtime: `user://profile.json` (not shipped).

### 3. Content notes
Remembered lines are first person, past tense, in the protagonists' voices; they only state what the player did
(the decision taken, the puzzle solved) — nothing new is invented. Dates are months („Januar 1964“, „Februar 1964“),
no invented days. No reward language; the finds counter is the same neutral counter as elsewhere.

## Asset manifest part — Chapter 3, part 2 „Der eiserne Frieden" (registry, fire, quay) — story stream, round 8

Date for all entries: **2026-11-06**. Everything below is original work for this project, written by hand or produced by
deterministic scripts in this repository; no downloaded images, sounds or samples, no AI image services. Licence:
project-owned, same as the game. Binding design: `documentation/C3P2_DESIGN.md`.

### Story, presentation and code
| Path | Kind | How it was made |
|---|---|---|
| `documentation/C3P2_DESIGN.md` | design contract | hand-written |
| `data/v3/*.json` (part-2 entries: 5 scenes, 5 quests, 19 objectives, 10 items, 7 conversations, `pz_c3_check`, 4 cinematics, 5 events), `data/v3/locale_de.json` (`world.c3c`–`c3g`, `obj.c3c_`–`obj.c3g_`, `choice.c3d.*`, `choice.c3g.*`, `item.c3_*` of part 2, `dlg.c3p2.*`) | story data, German texts | hand-written; applied to the JSON files by a one-off formatting script (byte-identical round trip of the existing records) |
| `data/chapters/c3.json` (levels, entry spawns, chapter cards, `switches`, palette `registry`, grades `registry`/`registry_fire`/`quay`, line expressions/variants, option texts, reactions, puzzle UI and cinematic registry of part 2) | presentation rules | hand-written |
| `data/locale/de_c3p2_dialogue.json`, `de_c3p2_cinematics.json`, `de_c3p2_ui.json` | German texts (variants, reactions, cinematic lines, check-puzzle UI, memory and task place names) | hand-written |
| `scripts/core/chapter_ext.gd` (`choice.<objective>` conditions, `level_key` / `same_level` / `scenes_of_level`) | GDScript | hand-written |
| `scripts/story/story_engine.gd` (`LOCKED_CINEMATICS` += 4 ids — validator list only) | GDScript | hand-written one-line extension |
| `scenes/levels/L_C3_Registry.tscn`, `L_C3_Quay.tscn` | level shells (story interactables, NPC nodes on named hotspots) | hand-written |
| `assets/environments/veltrava_registry/scene.json`, `assets/environments/veltrava_quay/scene.json` | **placeholder** art manifests (no images: spawns, hotspots, walk band, blockers) — replaced by the registry / quay art streams, which keep every name | hand-written |
| `assets/environments/veltrava_registry/explore_hotspots.json`, `assets/environments/veltrava_quay/explore_hotspots.json` | empty append-only targets for the exploration stream | hand-written |
| `scripts/core/autoplay.gd`, `scripts/core/inputplay.gd` (part-2 routes, check puzzle by keys, saves in `user://e2e/saves`) | E2E drivers | hand-written extensions |
| `tests/test_c3p2_routes.gd`, edits of the chain tests, `tests/support/test_kit.gd` | tests | hand-written |
| `tests/reference/make_python_reference.py` → `tests/reference/python_reference.json.gz` | parity fixture | generator extended by hand; fixture generated from the unmodified preproduction reference engine on `data/v3` |
| `tools/docs/gen_quest_db_c3.py` → `documentation/QUEST_DATABASE.md` §11 | documentation generator | extended by hand |

<a id="part-env_c3p2_archive"></a>

## Asset manifest part -- env_c3p2_archive (Chapter 3 part 2, the commission's registry, L_C3_Registry)

All files below are **original works generated procedurally** by the scripts in `tools/pixelgen/`
(`env_c3p2_archive.py` drives `env_c3p2_archive_room.py`, `_props.py`, `_fx.py`, helpers `env_c3p2_archive_kit.py`,
`env_c3p2_archive_layout.py`, the shared read-only `pg.py` and -- read-only -- the tone-space painter, light bake and
bitmap fonts of `env_veltrava_hall_kit.py`).  No pixels were copied, traced, sampled, downloaded or AI-generated; no
third-party assets, fonts or textures are embedded.

* Licence / rights: project-owned original artwork (generated for this project), freely redistributable with the
  game.  Provenance: deterministic generator (a re-run reproduces every file byte-for-byte; checked 2026-11-06),
  re-run with `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_c3p2_archive.py [--preview=DIR]`.  Machine-readable list:
  `assets/environments/veltrava_registry/provenance.json`.
* Date: 2026-11-06 (round 8).  Owner: registry art stream.
* Palette: `registry` of data/chapters/c3.json (night, slate, oak, lamp green, paper, brass, snow, fire, red, skin),
  checked at build time; every room look is palette-pure (tests/test_c3p2_archive_art.gd).
* The level (C3P2_DESIGN 5.1, side view 1920 x 360, feet y 300): the snowy Wirtschaftshof (gate with the
  trilingual delivery sign, the east-wing facade with one lit window, bracket lamp, the handcart with the emptied
  crates of wagon 4, the cast hatch "1909", ramp and the iron-clad ramp door), the Annahme (chute door "1909",
  oak standing desk with the intake book and the day's card box, red fire case with hose and axe, electric fire
  bell, the glazed porter's booth with key board, the 1909 Kurhaus plan, Bakelite radio, thermos and night book),
  the Karteisaal (bucket stand, Rohrpost receiver, rows of card cabinets with a rolling ladder, sorting table under
  the green lamp, stamp board, the card bank with drawer C-17 and Elena's wine-red scarf on the chair), the
  Planarchiv (office-green walls, the wall of framed Annex C sheets with Blatt 7 askew, plan chests "KORDON-17",
  drafting table) and the Magazin (bare brick, the enamel plate of the Kurhaus boiler house, mobile shelving with
  the C-17 unit, the cassation list, delegation shelves, the barred service-stair door with a steel fire door).
  A 1964 cellar without sprinklers: fire case, buckets, extinguisher, fire bell.
* Looks (one opaque room layer each, gated in scene.json, applied by scripts/levels/c3p2_archive/registry_level.gd):
  A night with Konrad's torch (until c3c_talk_konrad) - B the green lamp on - C smoke under the vaults, the Magazin
  and Planarchiv burning, the ramp door shut (after c3d_choose) - D the fire in the Karteisaal (after
  c3e_reach_konrad, cross-fades live).  Story patches: seal wire taken, scarf taken, halves on the table, the file
  taken, Blatt 7 pocketed (choice c3d_choose = pocket_plan), fire case smashed with the cut hose, desk pushed off
  the chute door.  FX: yard snow, snow through the door gaps, dust in the lamp light, flames (binary alpha +
  emission), embers and ash (no brightness flicker; ember fades are slower than 3 Hz), smoke veils, rolling smoke,
  plumes, heat shimmer, the ringing fire bell; life: a guard's boots passing a high window like a pendulum.
* Insignia (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE 12.4): `props/cart.png` / `props/cart_open.png` (railway stencil on the crates:
  neutral winged wheel + "МПС") and `patches/insignia_magazin_*.png` (delegation plates: fictional gear-and-wheat
  emblem / fictional Reichssiegel) are the neutral defaults; their `*_hist.png` twins carry the hammer and sickle
  and the hooked cross as small in-world stamps of the two delegations, chosen at runtime by `Symbols.pick()` --
  iconography of the regimes inside the critical narrative only, never decoration.
* Screenshots: documentation/screenshots/c3p2_archive_*.png (contact sheets for both protagonists and both symbol
  settings, the live C -> D cross-fade, the insignia comparison, seven full-size views).

<a id="part-env_c3p2_night"></a>

## Asset manifest part -- env_c3p2_night (Chapter 3 part 2, the night quay, L_C3_Quay)

All files below are **original works generated procedurally** by the scripts in `tools/pixelgen/`
(`env_c3p2_quay.py` drives `env_c3p2_quay_far.py`, `env_c3p2_quay_near.py` and `env_c3p2_quay_life.py`; the shared
helpers are `env_c3p2_quay_kit.py` and the read-only `env_veltrava_lib.py` / `pg.py`).  No pixels were copied, traced,
sampled, downloaded or AI-generated; no third-party assets, fonts or textures are embedded; the pixel fonts are the
hand-defined Veltrava fonts of `env_veltrava_lib.py`.

* Licence / rights: project-owned original artwork, freely redistributable with the game.  Provenance: deterministic
  generator with fixed seeds (a re-run reproduces every file byte for byte on the same Python/Pillow build), re-run
  with `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_c3p2_quay.py` (`--preview=DIR` adds CPU composites, no Godot lighting).
  Machine-readable list of every file: `assets/environments/veltrava_quay/provenance.json`.
  The generator never touches `assets/environments/veltrava_quay/explore_hotspots.json` (exploration stream).
* Date: 2026-11-06 (round 8).
* Design contract: `documentation/C3P2_DESIGN.md` §5.2 -- every spawn, hotspot and zone name/position of the story
  shell is kept (`bench` 760, `konrad_quay` 800, `typist_quay` 330, `guard_quay` 1050, `kessler_quay` 1180,
  `voronova_quay` 1330, `journalist_quay` 1560, `SP_C3_Quay` (560, 300); walk band y 296-306).
* Painting model: geometry is drawn as (material, tone, height) and one "night bake" (`env_c3p2_quay_kit.resolve`)
  applies the scene's light fields to every lit plane and prop (six balustrade lanterns, the pylon and shelter
  lamps, five burning souterrain windows, the engine's headlight): cold ladder under the night sky, warm ladder in
  lamplight, amber/red ladder in the fire light, all colours from the Veltrava palette of `data/chapters/c3.json`
  (+ its skin ramp) -- stable under the post-grade palette snap (grade `quay`).  Emissive glass/flames also write
  `_e` maps (added unshaded); `glow_air.png` is a soft additive halo layer.
* Palette: `veltrava` only (checked by the generator and by `tests/test_c3p2_env_art.gd`).
* Insignia (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.4): only the fender pennants of the two delegation cars.  `props/car_ussr.png`
  (neutral: red with the fictional gear-and-wheat badge) / `props/car_ussr_hist.png` (star, hammer and sickle);
  `props/car_reich.png` (neutral: grey with the fictional Reichssiegel) / `props/car_reich_hist.png` (the historical
  pennant).  Chosen at runtime by `Symbols.pick()`; in-world iconography of the regimes inside the critical narrative
  only, never on people.  No life strip has a `_hist` twin.
* Content: nobody is heroised, nobody is a caricature.  The firemen, the woken neighbours, the two aides and the
  two drivers who share one lighter are anonymous working people; the delegation heads and the cast are the level's
  NPCs (not painted).  No injury, no gore; the fire is shown through light, flames in the windows, smoke and steam.
* Accessibility: the searchlights sweep at 6.7 s / 8 s per sweep; flame strips change shape at <= 6 fps (flicker
  <= 3 Hz); the blue beacon turns once a second without a flash; Godot light flicker <= 0.22; nothing flashes the
  screen.

|---|---|---|

## Asset manifest part — c3p2_cine (Chapter 3 part 2: LS_C3_Radio, LS_C3_Fire, LS_C3_Escape, LS_C3_Halves)

* **Date:** 2026-11-06 (round 8; places and motion reworked in round 10, see the section below)
* **Area owner:** cinematics stream (C3P2_DESIGN.md §2.9); round 10: places stream (places, light, motion) and
  figures stream (every person: `env_c3p2_cine_figs` / `_hero` / `_people` / `_body`)
* **Scenes / code:** `scenes/cinematics/ch3_radio/` (`Ch3Radio.tscn`, `ch3_radio.gd`), `ch3_fire/`, `ch3_escape/`,
  `ch3_halves/` (the paths registered by the story stream in `data/chapters/c3.json` → `cinematics`) on the shared
  base `scenes/cinematics/c3p2_common/c3p2_cinematic.gd`, which extends the unchanged round-6 runtime
  `ch3_common/c3_cinematic.gd` (§12.5 contract: `finished` exactly once, idempotent `skip()`, `configure(ctx)`,
  `seek()`/`advance()`, cue list, reduced motion) with the c3p2 asset root, `choice(objective)` (the inner lines
  and the bench choreography follow `ctx.choices`: c3a/c3b in the radio film, c3d/c3g at the quay), a subtitle
  scheduler (lines follow each other, about-the-note lines after the note) and flame/puff/snow helpers; dev switch
  `--cine-choices=c3g_choose=swap_halves,…` for captures. Played by `ChapterExt.play_cinematic()`.
* **Generators:** `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_c3p2_cine.py [--only radio,fire,escape,halves]` (~45 s, figures
  cached) writes every texture and `assets/cinematics/c3p2/layout.json` (anchors shared with the runtimes: the
  dial/match cut, walker paths and footprints projected through the square's pinhole camera, flame anchors, hatch,
  window and board geometry; `tests/test_c3p2_cinematics.gd` checks them). Modules: `env_c3p2_cine_kit.py` (ramps,
  re-quantiser, glows, flame/smoke/ember sheets, pencil block letters), `env_c3p2_cine_radio.py`,
  `env_c3p2_cine_fire.py` (fire + escape), `env_c3p2_cine_quay.py` (halves), `env_c3p2_cine_figs.py`. Round-6 tools
  imported read-only: `env_veltrava_cine` (Paint, masks, light helpers), `env_veltrava_cine_kit` (trilingual pixel
  fonts, dithered puffs), `env_veltrava_cine_figs` (figure renderer), `chargen_portrait2_*` (the portrait specs).
  Sound: `py -3 -P tools/audiogen/c3p2_cine_audio.py` → `assets/audio/sfx/c3p2cine_*.wav` (on the DSP helpers of
  `c3_cine_audio.py`, read-only). Offline layer preview (review tool, not a test):
  `py -3 -P tests/visual/c3p2cine_preview.py OUT.png radio|fire|escape|halves`; in-game review with
  `tests/visual/c3cine_harness.tscn --c3-id=LS_C3_…` (shots / seq / run modes).
* **Figures:** Konrad and Alexei close up (at the radios without hats, at the sorting table, on the quay bench)
  are sculpted and lit with the signed-distance pipeline of the dialogue portraits through
  `env_veltrava_cine_figs.render()` with the specs `konrad()` / `alexei()` (faces, hats, hair, coats as in the
  portraits; seen from behind, lit by the dial, the green lamp or the fire). Smaller and action poses (crawling out
  of the hatch, lying in the snow, the hands, walking, the walkers on the square, Alexei backlit by the fire) are
  painted silhouettes with rim light. The arsonist is a backlit silhouette from behind in a long coat and porter's
  cap — never a face.
* **Determinism:** fixed seeds (`np.random.default_rng(n)`, `zlib.crc32(name)` for audio); two full builds are
  byte-identical (md5 over all 80 PNG + layout.json + 27 WAV, checked 2026-11-06).
* **Licence / provenance (all files below):** original procedural pixel art and synthesized audio made for ASHES OF
  EMPIRES: THE LAST WINTER by these generators (AI-authored code, project-owned, same licence as the game). No
  downloaded, copied or traced images, fonts or samples. The radio voices are wordless formant babble (the words are
  the subtitles). No insignia anywhere in these films (no `_hist` variants needed).
* **Palettes / grade:** night/snow/lamp ramps of the round-6 cinematics, the `registry` ramps of
  `data/chapters/c3.json` (oak, green lamp, paper, brass, fire), the protagonists' portrait ramps; grade `c3_cine`.
* **Names:** the German delegation's hotel is labelled „Hotel Europa“ as in the station level and LS_C3_Close (the
  design table says „Bellevue“; kept Europa for continuity — see the round-8 open issues).

### Round 10 — places, light and motion (user feedback: the films felt empty)
Same shots, order, lengths and lines; every place repainted as lit colour (albedo × light field + emission, snapped
to a scene palette with the kit's ordered dither) by new place modules, called from the round-8 build functions:
`env_c3p2_cine_life.py` (the float-RGB painter `Cv`, vectorised capsules, value noise at any coordinate, props:
bare lime trees, lamp posts, a snowed-in 1950s saloon, crates, barrels, railings, wires; sheets: steam lit from
below, fire smoke, living flame masses `flame_mass`, burning paper, crows, pigeons, the café cat),
`env_c3p2_cine_square.py` (r1/r6), `_rooms.py` (r2/r3), `_cafe.py` (r4/r5), `_registry.py` (f1–f4; the card hall is
ray-cast as a vaulted cellar), `_yard.py` (e1–e4), `_river.py` (h1–h4). People only through the figures stream's
API (`env_c3p2_cine_figs`: the hero figures, the drop-ins `walker`, `window_listener`, `doorway_figure`,
`fire_alexei`, `quay_walk`, `arm_alexei`/`hand_konrad`/`chute_file`/`hatch_konrad` when present, and the extras
`person_sheet`, `onlookers`, `firemen`); the h1 bench is drawn in their bench camera so the men sit on it.
* **Radio:** the square is layered (`square_bg` 664 × 380 at (−12, −10) with margins, `square_near` = the station
  roof's parapet with an urn and a lantern, `near_glow`): r1 cranes down onto it, r6 trucks right after the men
  (`square_camera(t)`); hotels with lit lobbies, balconies, brass and roof signs, random lit windows; tram wires and
  poles, a shelter, the clock post at 0.10, a kiosk, cars at the kerb, a taxi rank, ripples and tracks in the snow;
  steam of the 23.40 rises from behind the parapet (`steam` 6 × 150 × 96), a taxi drives the far street (`taxi`), a
  late couple crosses the near square (`couple_man`/`couple_woman`). Rooms: bed, print, radiator, frost flowers,
  curtains, newspaper (Nord); damask, wainscot, sconce, velvet curtains with tie-backs, net curtain, inkstand,
  folder, coffee cup with steam, the lamp's reflection (Europa); the chairs in front of the men (`chair_*`); a car's
  headlights sweep Alexei's ceiling. Doors: plaster/damask, chain, house rules, brass kick plate, rag rug, carpet,
  the keys with their fobs „214“/„317“ (`keyfob`, they shiver when the note pushes in), the courier's soles in the
  gaps; the note on the boards in the gap light with both folds. Café: front window with the reversed lettering
  and the closed sign, the station opposite with its name and lit clock (a running red second hand), lamp post,
  tram stop, pendant lamps, newspaper holders, coat stand with a forgotten umbrella, the bar with the espresso
  machine and night light, the cat on the bar (`cat`), bentwood chairs upside down, Elena's table in front
  (`cafe_fg`, parallax drift). Subtitles: the two broadcasts 7.0 / 6.5 s, the shared-note line 4.5 s, the note
  line 2.6 s — every line ≤ 19 characters per second (`tests/test_c3p2_cinematics.gd` now checks 19).
* **Fire:** f1/f4 ray-cast card hall (cabinets on three walls, segmental brick vault with tie rods, the barred
  window's snow light shaft, the ladder, the table with both halves, the red thread, magnifier, drawer C-17, ink,
  stamp, the chair with Elena's scarf); f2 aisle with caged lamps, pipes, ladder, book trolley, papers, the door's
  light wedge and the figure's long shadow down the aisle, smoke rolling along the vault (`smoke_hot`); f3 door
  with latch, hinges, crates, hand truck, fire bucket, lantern, the RAMPE sign, smoke creeping in; f4 with the
  ember band revealed behind the fire front (`fire_band`), new flame masses (`flame*.png`, `flame_tall`) on
  regenerated anchors, burning paper (`paper`) and a rolling smoke layer.
* **Escape:** chute with coal on the slide, soot, a pipe, the fire's glow from below and snow falling in; yard with
  the glowing souterrain window, drainpipe, coal heap, barrels, handcart, the gate and the lane's lamp; the lid
  sheds its snow; the hatch seen from above as a deep shaft with the glow below and sparks; the east wing with
  light wells, neighbouring houses, lime trees, a lamp post, a snowed-in car, flames licking out of the burst
  windows, glass glinting into the snow, onlookers (`onlooker_*`), two firemen running in (`fireman_*`) and a slow
  blue beacon as the bells come close.
* **Halves:** h1 far bank with the Line (double fence, barbed wire, towers, houses, lit windows), ice with drifts,
  ridges, cracks and the fire mirrored in streaks, the searchlights' pools wandering over the ice, the granite
  bench; h2 lime trees, globe lanterns, the bench and a bin, a parallax foreground (`h2_near`), lantern shadows
  under the walkers; h3 the palais with drum, dome and dormers, the burning east wing, the bridge with its lamps
  and the Line's barrier, a leaning smoke column with sparks, crows crossing the dawn (`crow`); h4 the station hall
  (glass roof at first light, chains, wall lamps), pigeons on the board (`pigeon`), steam (`steam`), two travellers
  looking up (`traveller_*`).
* **Checks:** the c3p2 suites pass (7 files, 86 cases); insignia on/off give identical frames in all four films
  (no insignia; the scripts never read `symbols`).

### Round 11 — art-director pass (fire that reads as fire, soft smoke, readable beats)
Same shots, order, lengths and story; new effect generators in `env_c3p2_cine_life.py`: `soft_puff_sheet` (soft
volumetric puffs — little lit spheres, alpha in a few levels, frayed rims — instead of the ordered-dither puffs that
read as checkerboard blotches), `soft_plume` (a whole smoke column as one soft image), `fire_sheet` (licking tongues
from a burning bed, domain-warped turbulence that only eats into the fire, flicks; colour bands from a translucent
red fringe to a white-hot core — no dark rim, the core is the brightest pixel), `fire_halo` (additive bloom).
* **Fire:** f4's hall is darker (`f4_bg` = the hall before the fire reaches it); the fire's light is `f4_lit`
  (burning hall minus unlit hall, additive) revealed behind the running front with a soft edge; the table is a
  layer in front (`f4_table`), so the clusters (`fire_mid`/`fire_big`/`fire_wide`/`fire_small`, anchors and kinds in
  layout `fire.f4_fire` / `f4_kinds`) rise behind its far edge, merge into one wall of uneven height, catch small and
  grow; no flames float alone over the cornices; halos bloom around them; soft smoke (`smoke_hot`) rolls along the
  vault toward the camera. f2: the aisle is dark before the fire (`f2_glow` = the fire's light, faded in from the
  catch), the boxes burn with `f2_fire_s/_m/_l` and halos, soft smoke rolls along the aisle's vault. f3: the gap
  light varies along its length (the yard lamp, snowflakes passing) and spills cold light on the jamb and the floor;
  the snow blown in is cold white. f1: soft lamp-lit wisps.
* **Radio:** the steam of the 23.40 is soft (`steam`, 6 × 150 × 110) and billows up from behind the parapet in
  beats; the lamp pools are lamp light on snow (pale warm greys, sparkle) plus soft additive pools; r6 pushes in
  toward the dark east wing, the men step out at a readable size (`walker_*`, 21/20 px, rigged through
  `env_c3p2_cine_people.person`) with their door-light shadows, then a dissolve (`LATER`) to a minute on: two
  trodden furrows with footprints (layout `trail_*`, `prints_*`, `path_*` over the whole way, `walk_*` metres)
  converge across the snow and the men, small (`walker_far_*`), reach the wing; the headlight sweep lands on the
  walls and the ceiling's edge, never on the glass; the folded note reads as a folded sheet; the café has a worn
  low-contrast floor with the window's light and sheen, a second row of tables, a mop bucket, a mat, a chair in
  front, readable bentwood chairs, a lever espresso machine, a passing car's light, and a visible parallax drift.
  Subtitles: the broadcasts are condensed excerpts (72 characters, 3.8 s each, same meaning: Anlage C, „begleitete
  Personen“ vs. „Begleitgut … ohne Halt“) so every line plays over its own picture (`LINE_AT`; checked by
  `radio_lines_over_their_pictures`).
* **Escape:** the lid is opaque cast iron (`e2_hatch`: tread plate under melting snow with the cast date; lifting
  with its thick edge; open with its ribbed underside, hinge knuckles, soot); the lane gets columns of soft smoke
  from every burst window merging into a mass under the cornice (snow falls in front), flames licking out
  (`flame_out`), halos, glass shards that glint and stay twinkling in the snow, onlookers who react (take sequences
  in layout `escape.e4_people[].seq`: flinch, hands to the face, a step back, pointing, walking to the engine) and
  the fire engine nosing in (`engine`) with its turning blue beacon (`beacon`, `beacon_wash`).
* **Halves:** h3's smoke column is the soft `h3_plume` (no flat sheet, no holes) drifting east; the dawn is visible
  (`h3_dawn`: the sky lifts, a pale band and a rose-grey line on the right horizon; horizon ≈ RGB 13,17,26 → 80,86,112);
  soft `smoke`, `steam`, `breath`; window fires from `fire_sheet`.

### Round 11 — figures (art-director pass: anatomy, no paper dolls)
Figures stream (`env_c3p2_cine_body` / `_hero` / `_people` / `_figs` / `_hands`).  Rig: a short, strong neck (the
head sits 6 units lower, the neck ~0.7 of the head's width, the trapezius high and flat — no bottle shoulders),
collars resized to it; skin ramps per scene light with warm shadows (`skin_ramp`, no lilac / mauve); cropped hair
with strands from the crown and a soft hairline at the nape (`pass_hair_strands`, `hair_fade`); anatomical sleeves
(deltoid / biceps mass, a slim elbow, the forearm tapering to the cuff, folds only in the bend); a larger palm;
the close shots trace a deeper volume (a hand far up a desk was cut away by the old ±90 px one).  An arm that moves
is never also part of its body sprite: its sheet holds it in every frame and is drawn under the body.
* **Radio:** `arm_alexei` 6 frames (resting on the table, lifting, on the way × 2, on the knob, turning; runtime
  `ARM_KEYS`), Alexei leans toward the set; `hand_konrad` 18 frames — Konrad writes with his LEFT hand and a pencil
  (the pad lies at his front-left): 6 positions × 2 strokes, then the 6 positions with the pencil lifted; the
  runtime picks the position the growing line has passed and shifts the sprite the rest of the way
  (`_apply_pencil_hand`), the line itself (`pad_line`, from `env_c3p2_cine_figs.PAD_LINE`) runs on the part of the
  notepad the radio does not cover.
* **Fire:** `f1_konrad` 4 frames (head bowed, lifting, up, turned toward the smoke; runtime `LOOK_KEYS`), the whole
  seated man on an archive chair down to the frame's bottom, the overcoat a dark slate navy under the green lamp.
* **Escape:** `e1_file` — the forearms come in from the lower corners, the hands lie flat on the file's back with
  spread fingers and thumbs; `e2_konrad` 6 frames (shoulders, waist, a knee on the rim, on all fours beside the
  hatch, two crawl steps — no jump; runtime `KONRAD`); `e3a_hands` 7 frames (the hand rises, opens, Konrad's open
  hand comes in, closes round the wrist, the pull in three steps; runtime `HANDS`); `e3b_men` from a low camera
  (profiles, lying in the snow with an uneven lip, no rim outline).
* **Halves:** `fig_alexei` / `fig_konrad` 4 frames (breath × 2, leaning over half / fully for the far stack);
  `arms` 2 rows × `arm_cols` (14) cells of 248 × 132 at `arms_at`: reach in three steps near / far, the pull with the
  pages (two cells each; the runtime shifts the sprite with the sliding stack), the hold, Konrad's list in his hand
  beside his hip (3 cells; the fold is drawn at layout `list_fold_at`); runtime `_apply_arms`.

### Checks
`tests/test_c3p2_cinematics.gd` (21 cases, ~1190 checks): registration; contract and planned lengths; `finished`
exactly once (skip/skip, skip before play, natural end, fast-forward, replay); inner lines for every c3a×c3b and
c3d×c3g combination, spoken in order, non-overlapping, inside the film, ≤ 19 characters per second, German text
present, every radio line over its own picture (round 11); skip stops the cues; reduced/full motion; shot order; no missing texture and layout keys in sync with the
scripts (dial = match cut); every cued sound exists and every loop is stopped; sound/picture timings shared with the
audio generator (lamp dips, chute shoves, window bursts); frames a pure function of t (seek away and back); the match
cut on the dial; who takes which pages per c3g; reduced motion halves the travel; fire light never strobes (≤ 3 Hz,
small frame-to-frame changes). In-game: real-time runs 30.8 / 12.0 / 16.0 / 21.0 s wall time, `finished` once,
~4.2 ms frames (harness `--c3-mode=run`).

## Asset manifest part — Chapter 3 part 2, joint evidence check „Gegenprobe“ (puzzle UI stream, round 8)

Date for all entries: **2026-11-06**. Everything below is original work for this project, produced by a deterministic
generator in this repository or written by hand; no downloaded images, fonts or sounds, no AI image services, no tracing
of references. Licence: project-owned, same as the game. Contract: `documentation/C3P2_DESIGN.md` §2.7.

### Board art — `tools/pixelgen/gen_ui_c3p2.py` (`py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_ui_c3p2.py [--preview=DIR]`)
Drawn 1:1, shown at x3 with nearest filtering; text-free (every word is set in HD by the UI from Loc keys). Palette: the
UI / archive ramps of `gen_ui_c3.py` (paper, ink, red, slate, amber) — no new hues. Fixed seeds, identical output on
every run.

| `assets/ui/puzzle/c3p2_mark_<row>.png` | „matching marks“ shown after the solution | procedural pencil strokes (loops, underlines) around the shared fact of each pair, positioned from the same `marks` metadata; red pencil on paper, amber chalk on the black departure board |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c3p2_card_in_c17_44.png`, `…_out_2340.png`, `…_par_4.png`, `…_january.png` | registry index cards (the tray) | procedural: dithered paper, red series edge, glyph box with a distinct shape glyph (ring / cross / star / bowtie — never colour coded) |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c3p2_slot.png` | empty row slot | procedural (same language as `c3_slot.png`) |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c3p2_bracket.png` | red pencil brace over the four rows (Kordon-17) | procedural curve |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c3p2_board.json` | sizes, text rects, strip sources, mark rects, layout constants, measured surface colours, solution self-check | written by the generator |

<a id="part-c3p2_cast_audio"></a>

## Asset manifest — Chapter 3 part 2: cast looks and sound (round 8)

Area owner: c3p2 cast/audio specialist · Date: **2026-11-06, round 8** · Engine target: Godot 4.7.2
Contract: `documentation/C3P2_DESIGN.md` §6 (characters: existing ids only, a soot look for the fire scenes and the
quay, a carry row with the file, Frau Brunner in her coat over the nightdress) and §8 (audio). Extends
`characters_r7.md`, `portraits_r7.md` and `c3.md` (Veltrava sound); everything listed there stays valid.

### Provenance & licence (applies to every file below)
* **Original work, generated procedurally** by the project's own Python scripts (numpy + Pillow; no AI models, no
  network, no samples, no third-party art or recordings, no image or audio inputs).
* Characters: the looks are rendered by the project's own paper-doll rig and portrait pipeline from the base
  characters' own specs (same poses, same heads) plus hand-chosen soot ramps, smear positions and costume parts.
* Sound: synthesised (noise shaping, modal impacts, stick-slip creaks, additive instruments, generated impulse
  responses). Voices are unintelligible by construction (prosody profiles over random vowel targets — no words, no
  language, no real broadcast). The night waltz and the interval signal are original compositions (the interval
  signal is the game's own two-language motif V). The 1964 brigade horns are a generic two-tone of a fourth, not a
  recording or a brand.
* Licence: project-owned original assets, no attribution needed. No insignia on any new sprite.
* Deterministic: every generator reproduces byte-identical files (verified 2026-11-06: all 32 WAVs rebuilt and
  md5-compared; all 18 look files rebuilt into a scratch directory and byte-compared).

### Reproduce
```bash
py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/characters_c3p2.py --portraits   # 3 looks: sheets, normal maps, living portraits, checks
py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/characters_c3p2.py --check       # validators only (characters_check + look contract)
py -3 -P tools/audiogen/c3p2_audio.py                    # 32 WAVs + .import + analysis + numeric checks
py -3 -P tools/audiogen/c3p2_audio.py --list             # names and where each is wired
## then the shared, locked import (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §1)
```
**Re-run `characters_c3p2.py --portraits` whenever `alexei`, `konrad` or `typist` (sheet or portrait) change** —
the looks derive from them and must keep the same silhouette (checked).

### Characters — looks (`tools/pixelgen/chargen_cast_c3p2.py`, `chargen_portrait_c3p2.py`, runner `characters_c3p2.py`)
A *look* is a sheet directory, not a character: dialogue speaker and NPC `char_id` stay `alexei` / `konrad` /
`typist`; the runtime picks the directory per scene (`data/chapters/c3.json` `"looks"` — integration notes, round 8).

| Directory | Base | What | Rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| `assets/characters/alexei_soot/` | `alexei` | after the smoke: blackened coat hem (ragged 2–4 px band under a 3-px dust band), sooty cuffs and hands, smoke-grey face with two dark smears (cheek, jaw), coal dust on the legs, greyed scarf, two ash flakes on the ushanka. Silhouette pixel-identical to `alexei` in every shared row | the 15 rows of `alexei` (same indices) + `carry_side` (8-frame walk, the file C-17 clamped under the near arm, ground speed = walk_side 34.5 px/s) + `carry_idle_side` (8-frame idle with the file) |
| `assets/characters/konrad_soot/` | `konrad` | the same for Konrad (forehead smear under the brim, beige scarf greyed, ash on the hat); his folder and tube stay | 15 rows of `konrad` + the two carry rows (35.0 px/s) |
| `assets/characters/typist_night/` | `typist` | Frau Brunner at the quay, 2.20 at night: dark aubergine-brown wool coat (knee length, collar up) over the pale nightdress (hem to the ankles), nightdress collar at the throat, felt boots, near hand holding the coat shut (gesture: hand to the collar); no pencil, no pad, no sleeve protectors | the 9 rows of `typist` |

Each directory: `sheet.png`, `sheet_n.png` (normal map), `sheet.json` (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §4 contract, base rows
first), `portrait.png` (4 × 112 strip), `portrait_anim.png` (living portrait grid, 6 rows) and `portrait.json`
(round-2 contract + `"look_of": <base id>`). Portraits: soot smears placed in head-local coordinates (they follow every
expression), grimy collar, ash on the hat, the rim light turned warm (the fire behind them), a smoky backdrop glowing
from the left (`c3p2_smoke`); `typist_night` on the quay's night backdrop (`c3p2_quay`) with the fire's glow.
Backdrops are registered in memory in `chargen_portrait2_paint.BG` — no `chargen_portrait2*.py` file is changed.
Review images: `assets/characters/_preview/c3p2_*` (git-ignored, `.gdignore`).

### Sound (`tools/audiogen/c3p2_audio.py`) — 44.1 kHz / 16-bit PCM, lossless import, loops carry a `smpl` chunk
Measured on the delivered files (RMS / sample peak in dBFS):

| File | Content | Length | RMS / peak | Wired |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `amb/amb_veltrava_registry.wav` | souterrain at night: cellar room tone, the city far off, wind and snow at the high windows, radiator ticks + one gurgle, the porter's radio low in the glazed booth (the night waltz), a guard's steps crossing the yard snow above, the house settling, one far goods train | 60 s loop, stereo | −26.0 / −8.8 | `L_C3_Registry.tscn` ambience (scenes A, B) |
| `amb/amb_veltrava_fire.wav` | the registry burning: breathing roar, crackle (tamed around 3 kHz so the text stays in front), ember hiss, sap squeals, timber groaning and splitting, heat-cracked panes, debris far off, the palais' electric fire bell muffled through brick (10-s spells, 5-s pauses), the brigade's hand bell outside once | 60 s loop, stereo | −24.3 / −3.0 | `c3.json` switches C / D `"ambience"` |
| `amb/amb_veltrava_quay.wav` | the quay before dawn: wind over the frozen river, the burning wing far left, the ladder engine's pump idling, hose spray, river ice singing (dispersive chirps) and booming, the engine's bell, a second brigade's two-tone horn far across the town, boots in the snow | 60 s loop, stereo | −24.0 / −3.0 | `L_C3_Quay.tscn` ambience |
| `music/mus_c3p2_fire.wav` | tense, not action: bowed A drone, a heartbeat pulse that leans on Bb in the middle, violas rising A→Bb→B→C and falling back, flickering string harmonics, low brass breathing, the V motif begun and broken off twice; no drums | 66.7 s loop (72 bpm, 80 beats), stereo | −21.0 / −6.9 | to wire: `c3.json` switches C / D `"music"` (integration notes) |
| `music/mus_c3p2_halves.wav` | quiet: the two-language motif V divided — piano first half / viola second half (with Veltrava's Bb), then swapped (piano with B), then the piano alone and no answer; open-fifth drone, slow pads, celesta snow | 102.9 s loop (56 bpm, 96 beats), stereo | −23.0 / −5.1 | `L_C3_Quay.tscn` music (LS_C3_Halves keeps it) |
| `sfx/c3p2_fire_low/_mid/_high.wav` | fire beds in three intensities (smouldering crackle → steady fire → a room ablaze) | 20 s loops, stereo | −33.4 / −26.4 / −21.6, peaks −3.0 | cinematics (`loop` cue), level scripts (`Audio.sfx_loop`) |
| `sfx/c3p2_yard_night.wav` | the snowy yard at night: soft wind, flakes, the bracket lamp's hum, the town, a far train | 30 s loop, stereo | −27.0 / −6.4 | registry yard (level script) / LS_C3_Escape bed |
| `sfx/c3p2_radio_waltz.wav` | the porter's radio close up (same waltz, valve-set speaker) | 60 s loop, mono | −24.0 / −12.7 | `exp_radio` / registry level script |
| `sfx/c3p2_radio_interval.wav` | Radio Veltrava's interval signal (V on a music box, twice) | 4.0 s mono | −19.3 / −8.0 | LS_C3_Radio r1 (optional; the cinematic has its own `c3p2cine_*`) |
| `sfx/c3p2_radio_news_ru.wav`, `_de.wav` | newsreader murmur through the set (two prosody profiles, no words) | 6.5 s mono | −20.8 / −9.0 | LS_C3_Radio r2 / r3 (optional) |
| `sfx/c3p2_radio_tune.wav` | the dial: static, heterodyne whistle, fragments, the carrier settles | 1.4 s mono | −21.4 / −10.0 | LS_C3_Radio match cut (optional) |
| `sfx/c3p2_note_slide.wav` | a folded note pushed under a door | 1.7 s | −32.9 / −12.0 | LS_C3_Radio r4 |
| `sfx/c3p2_fire_catch.wav` | the C-17 stacks catching: whoomph, paper crackling up, first roar | 5.4 s | −27.4 / −6.0 | LS_C3_Fire f2 |
| `sfx/c3p2_bolt.wav` | the ramp door's iron bolt drawn home outside (heard through oak) | 3.6 s | −30.8 / −5.0 | LS_C3_Fire f3, `c3e_try_ramp` |
| `sfx/c3p2_desk_push.wav` | the oak intake desk dragged off the chute door | 4.4 s | −23.2 / −5.0 | `dlg_c3f_desk` („Drei!“) |
| `sfx/c3p2_door_jam.wav` | the jammed 1909 iron door: latch, grinding, a shoulder, a few centimetres | 4.0 s | −28.7 / −5.0 | `dlg_c3f_chute` |
| `sfx/c3p2_hatch_open.wav`, `_slam.wav` | the coal hatch in the yard: rusty hinge, lid on the snowy stone; slammed on the smoke | 6.0 / 5.7 s | −31.5 / −4.0, −28.7 / −3.0 | LS_C3_Escape e2 / e3 |
| `sfx/c3p2_window_burst.wav` | the souterrain windows bursting, glass on the yard, flame breathing out | 6.7 s | −25.3 / −4.0 | LS_C3_Escape e4 |
| `sfx/c3p2_roof_creak_1..3.wav` | burning timber under load (the third ends splitting) | 4.6–6.0 s | −27…−30 / −7…−9 | fire scenes (random variant via `Audio.sfx("c3p2_roof_creak")`) |
| `sfx/c3p2_glass_1..3.wav` | heat-cracked panes and falling shards | 4.8 s | −40…−43 / −6.0 | fire scenes (`Audio.sfx("c3p2_glass")`) |
| `sfx/c3p2_fire_bell.wav` | the ladder engine's brass bell rung by hand across the yard | 7.6 s | −23.2 / −6.0 | LS_C3_Escape e4, quay |
| `sfx/c3p2_sirens_far.wav` | two brigades' two-tone horns of 1964 crossing the town far away | 10 s | −23.6 / −9.0 | LS_C3_Escape e4, LS_C3_Halves h3 |

Level windows enforced by the generator and `tests/test_c3p2_cast_audio.gd`: ambiences −28…−21 dBFS RMS, peak ≤ −2.5;
music −25…−19, peak ≤ −0.8; sfx loops −36…−19, peak ≤ −2.5; one-shots −45…−9, peak ≤ −2.5, ending in silence.
Loops: `smpl` loop = whole file (imported as `LOOP_FORWARD`, checked), level continuous across the loop point
(|Δ RMS over 50 ms| ≤ 3 dB, the seam step an ordinary step; measured: registry 0.4 dB / 1.2× median step, fire 1.2 dB
/ 2.7×, quay 0.8 dB / 1.0×, music 0.1 and 0.6 dB, fire beds ≤ 0.9 dB / ≤ 2.1×, yard 1.9 dB / 0.5×, waltz 1.8 dB / 0.2×). Spectral balance was checked by band shares (the quay and the yard
were de-hissed: 4–8 kHz share 21 % → 5 %; the fire ambience's 2–4 kHz crackle band 20 % → 11 %).
Spectrograms + `c3p2_analysis.json`: `assets/audio/_preview/` (git-ignored).

### Tests
`tests/test_c3p2_cast_audio.gd` — looks follow their base (rows, indices, fps, variants, silhouette, visible soot,
carry rows and their ground speed), load and play on `SpriteRig`, living portraits (contract, talk/blink rows,
differ from the base); every WAV's format, loop chunk, import loop mode and length; levels and seams measured on the
PCM; wiring (level ambience/music ids, switch settings, an optional `"looks"` table, every c3p2 sound a round-8
cinematic cue names).

## Asset manifest — Chapter 3 part 2 exploration: registry and quay finds, „Die Pforte“, „Die Suchkartei“ (area: explore_c3p2)

Owner: exploration c3p2 specialist · Date: 2026-11-06 · Engine: Godot 4.7.2 · Contract: documentation/C3P2_DESIGN.md §7,
TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.1/§12.3/§12.8/§12.9 (user request this round: Chapter 3 part 2, content, look, feel, fun).

Everything listed here is **original work**, written for this game or generated procedurally by the project scripts
below on 2026-11-06. No pixel, sample or melody was taken from `documentation/references/` or any other source; no
downloads, no AI media, no recordings. Colours come from `data/chapters/c3.json` palette `registry` (night, slate, oak,
lamp_green, paper, brass, snow, red) plus one documented extra: the violet office ink of the Russian clerks' cards
(`VIOLET` #3E2C5E / #5C4488 / #8068B0 in the generator). Pixel fonts: the hand-made bitmaps of
`tools/pixelgen/mg_r7_v_kit.py` (SMALL 5×7, Latin + Cyrillic) and `tools/pixelgen/env_veltrava_lib.py` (FONT_S 3×5,
Latin + Cyrillic) — no font file is read or traced. No regime insignia anywhere (§12.4): the tracing index is the
neutral commission's, its drawer carries only „SUCHKARTEI“. Historical model: 1950s/60s office card-index cabinets
(oak drawers, brass label holders, card rods, guide cards with coloured tabs) and Soviet office practice of violet ink.
Licence: original project work (same terms as the rest of the game).

### Content (text, data)

| File | What |
|---|---|
| `data/explore/c3p2_registry.json` | 18 registry entries (level key `L_C3_RegistryA`): Alexei, scene A — gate sign, two tracks (secret), handcart, coal hatch 1909, his own index card („Begleitperson“, card `hist.soviet.filtration`), the side task „Die Pforte“ (thermos → radio + ten-Kronen note → key board → the porter's note); Konrad, scene B — fire bucket, his own card („Anfrage Berlin“, card `hist.reich.census_1939`), rolling ladder, stamps and seal pliers „Zange 7“, the tracing index (mini-game), boots at the window, plan chests (Blatt 7 missing), Kesselhaus 1909 plaque, cassation list („Serie C-17 … erledigt“) |
| `data/explore/c3p2_quay.json` | 10 quay entries (Alexei): two soot hands (secret), the ladder engine (Storz/Bogdanov adapter), lantern, ice, searchlights, two cars and one lighter, the bridge of the city arms; chats with Frau Brunner, Lidia Voronova, Nils Ekdahl |
| `data/explore/c3p2_history_reich.json`, `c3p2_history_soviet.json` | the two real-history cards with their sources (fact-checked 06.11.2026, ≥ 2 independent web sources each + literature) |
| `data/locale/de_explore_c3p2.json`, `de_history_c3p2.json` | all German texts (finds, chats, task, mini-game, place labels; the two cards) |
| `assets/environments/veltrava_registry/explore_hotspots.json`, `veltrava_quay/explore_hotspots.json` | the exp_* hotspots (shared, append-only blocks) |

| Asset | Generator | What |
|---|---|---|
| `assets/ui/minigames/kartei/kartei_back.png` (400×136) | `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_mg_c3p2_kartei.py` | the card-hall floor at night (oak parquet, the green lamp's pool, cold edges) with the fallen drawer: back wall, dark inner bottom, side walls, shadow |

| `kartei_tabs.png` (15 × 22×36) | same | guide cards „B·Б“, „K·К“, „V·В“ (pressboard, coloured tabs) and the upper part of the twelve cards (origin letter, year/number, handwriting) |

| `kartei_closeup.png` (200×120) | same | close-up for the find (DiscoveryView): the drawer in order, Teodor Wanek's card („Anschrift übermittelt“) on top |

Scripts (no assets): `scripts/ui/minigames/kartei_game.gd` (modal, extends `minigame_base.gd`: auto mode, `demo_solve()`
by synthetic keys, hints after two refusals following Settings `puzzle_hints`, Esc puts a held card back or gives up,
keyboard + pad + mouse, compact ×2 layout above 100 % text, high-contrast cursor), `kartei_logic.gd` (pure rule),
`kartei_spot.gd` (registry find spot: Story gates in the game, the remembered scene in memories), `kartei_world_cue.gd`.
Deterministic: seeds are crc32 of names; re-running the generators reproduces the files byte for byte.

Method: `walkers_r8.py` calls the existing cast function (`chargen_cast` / `chargen_cast_new` / `chargen_cast_c3`) with a
few entries of its material table swapped (same keys and order → same material indices) and renders the rows of the
shipped `sheet.json` through `chargen_body.render`. The tool checks that the unmodified pipeline reproduces every source
sheet byte for byte (`--check`) and that every variant's silhouette is identical to its source, so the runtime reuses
the character's own `sheet_n.png` and `sheet.json`; only the diffuse changes. Runtime: `scripts/actors/ambient_walker.gd`
+ `scenes/actors/AmbientWalkers.tscn` (one actor entry in each `scene.json`).

`assets/characters/voronova/sheet.png` (only this file changed; `sheet_n.png` and `sheet.json` are identical): the
lowest row of the karakul pirozhok uses the light curl-tip tones (hat ramp 5/4 instead of 1/2) in the side and front
head bitmaps, a 1-px lighter fur edge so the hat no longer merges with her grey hair at 1×. Patched in memory by

`py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/characters_c3.py voronova`.

`assets/cinematics/ch3/trains/int_alexei.png` and `roomflare_a.png` only (`py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_veltrava_cine.py
--only t3`): a pleated pelmet with scalloped hem and red cross-stitch band, curtain tie-backs and lace edges, the lower
berths with leatherette buttons and piping, made-up bedding on the right berth (folded blanket with the red stripe,
white pillow, paper-wrapped linen), Alexei's kit bag, folded blankets on the upper berths, a carpet runner, a second
(empty) tea glass in a filigree holder with the spoon standing in it; the room flare of the passing station lamp now
throws the warm, skewed window (with the sash shadow) over the right curtain and wall and a band across the ceiling.
The glass opening is untouched (t2 match cut), timing unchanged (25.5 s).

## Asset manifest part -- env_c4_tunnels (Chapter 4 part 1, Alexei's Karsk: Archivkeller, Dienststollen 3, Umsetzgleis, L_C4_Tunnels)

All files below are **original works generated procedurally** by the scripts in `tools/pixelgen/`
(`env_c4_tunnels.py` drives `env_c4_tunnels_room.py` (cellar, door wall, floors), `env_c4_tunnels_siding.py`
(earth section, tunnel, siding hall, wagon 24 318), `env_c4_tunnels_fx.py` (outside, veils, strips, swing lamps,
event strips); `env_c4_tunnels_docs.py` (inspect documents); helpers `env_c4_tunnels_kit.py`,
`env_c4_tunnels_layout.py`, and -- read-only -- `pg.py`, the tone-space painter / light bake / bitmap fonts of
`env_veltrava_hall_kit.py`, the masonry / wood painters of `env_c3p2_archive_kit.py` and the hand-pixelled emblem
masks of `env_karsk_emblems.py`).  No pixels were copied, traced, sampled, downloaded or AI-generated; no third-party
assets, fonts or textures are embedded.  References (Soviet two-axle covered wagons, 1941 air-raid shelters, lever
frames, freight-office ledgers) were studied only, never copied.

* Licence / rights: project-owned original artwork, freely redistributable with the game.  Provenance: deterministic
  generators (two consecutive runs reproduce all 72 files byte-for-byte, checked 2026-11-06); re-run with
  `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_c4_tunnels.py [--preview=DIR]` and `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_c4_tunnels_docs.py`,
  then the shared `--import`.  Machine-readable list: `assets/environments/karsk_tunnels/provenance.json` (62 files).
* Date: 2026-11-06 (round 9).  Owner: env-tunnels stream.
* Palette `tunnels` = data/chapters/c4.json `palettes.tunnels` (night, brick, iron, lamp, wagon, paper, snow, red,
  skin -- kept unchanged) plus two ramps taken from Chapter 1's Karsk palette so Karsk stays one city:
  **`green`** `#1B3033 #26403F #36534D #4A675C #6C7A62 #9DAF91` (oil-paint dado, Lev's lamp shade, the iron door) and
  **`concrete`** `#2A2D2C #3B3F3B #4F534C #666A60 #7F8275 #9A9B8B` (the 1941 tube, the siding hall).  Every room layer,
  patch and document is palette-pure in it (`tests/test_c4_tunnels_art.gd`); scene.json `meta.palette_ramps` lists it.

| Path (assets/environments/karsk_tunnels/ unless noted) | Content |
|---|---|
| `room.png` (+ `_n`, `_e`) | the whole level, baked once (1920 x 360): **Archivkeller** (scene A, ~4.40) -- four brick barrel vaults on lunettes, two barred windows, the stair from the office (door ajar, warm), the freight-book shelves 1963 / 1964 / АРХИВ with the January gap and Lev's slip, stamp rack, card cabinet, wall clock (4.40), 1964 calendar, railway schematic „СХЕМА / КАРСК“, Lev's desk (green lamp, open January book, abacus, photo, tea), samovar table, the light box made of a signal lamp, the stove with felt boots, the 1941 iron door with „Бомбоубежище · Служебный тоннель №3“; the **door wall** in section; **Dienststollen 3** (scene C) -- earth section with the freight yard above, concrete tube, cage lamps, old and new cables, heating pipe with the leaking flange, phone niche, „← К укрытию“, lever frame (8 levers, diagram board) and point „СТР 2“, the sealed gallery „ЗАКРЫТО“, boards with a scratched house; the **Umsetzgleis** -- vaulted hall, the siding office „ДЕЖУРНЫЙ“ with the card box „C-17/45 / ПРИНЯТЬ“ and the stamp, the painters' crate and roster post, the paint stand (C-17/41 … /45, two alphabets, the bucket), fire shield, plate heap, wagon 24 318 (C-17/45 stencil still taped over 47 702 over the ghost of C-17/42, enamel plate 24 318, chalk „ВТ 23.40“, marks „РЕВ 11.62 / ДЕП КАРСК“), the kit pile, the brazier, the shut portal with padlock and dawn in the cracks |
| `patches/plan_taken`, `door_open`, `wagon_open` (+ maps) | opaque crops of a bake with one story change: the tunnel plan handed over (after `c4a_talk_tunnel`), the iron door open (after `c4a_open_door`), the wagon door slid open (after `c4c_inspect_wagon`) |
| `patches/insignia_calendar`, `insignia_lever_plate`, `insignia_wagon` (+ `_hist`, maps) | the three insignia sites: fictional gear-and-wheat emblem of Chapter 1 / hammer and sickle in the `_hist` twin (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE §12.4, Symbols.pick) |
| `outside.png` | the yard at night behind the cellar windows (snow at the glass, the yard lamp, a wagon) |
| `veils.png` | soft light cones (bulb over the wagon, work lamps, the enamel lamp, Lev's lamp) and the faint paint haze |
| `fg_frame.png` | dark foreground silhouettes, parallax 1.25 (crates, sack, cable drum, buffer stop, sleepers) |
| `fx/snow_win_0/1`, `feet_win`, `samovar_steam`, `stove_vents` (+`_e`), `clock_pendulum`, `dust_lamp`, `dust_hang` | life of scene A: snow behind the windows, the night shift's felt boots passing, steam, the fire breathing, the pendulum, dust in the lamplight |
| `fx/drip_0..4`, `steam_flange`, `booth_smoke`, `kerosene_flame` (+`_e`), `dust_bulb`, `dust_work`, `brazier_embers` (+`_e`), `snow_gate` | life of scene C: drops falling into puddles, steam at the flange, smoke from the booth's pipe, the kerosene flame, dust in the bulb and work-lamp light, embers, snow blown under the gate |
| `fx/hang_lamp`, `fx/bulb_wagon` (+`_e`) | 7-frame swing strips of the two hanging lamps (driven by the level script) |
| `fx/dust_fall_0..7`, `fx/train_top` | event strips: grit shaken from the vault, the underframes of a train crossing the yard above |
| `scene.json`, `provenance.json` | art manifest (story contract kept: spawns, hotspots, npcs, the blocker `cellar_door`) + file list |
| `assets/ui/documents/karsk_tunnels_book_gap`, `_freight_book`, `_carbon`, `_plan`, `_tracks`, `_card_ru`, `_stencils`, `_wagon_inside` (.png) + `karsk_tunnels_documents.json` | inspect close-ups of the eight Karsk items of C4_DESIGN §2.5 (weighbill style, legible only the story figures; the card's perforation contract pitch 6 / hole 3 / first 8 px / 15 holes is shared with the German half) |
| `data/locale/de_env_karsk_tunnels.json` | German caption lines (alt texts) of the eight documents |

Code and tests of this stream (hand-written): `scripts/levels/c4/tunnels_level.gd` (extends the registry level:
scene filters, camera zones, looks, swinging lamps, the passing train), the root script line of
`scenes/levels/L_C4_Tunnels.tscn`, `tests/test_c4_tunnels_art.gd`, `tests/visual/c4_tunnels_shots.gd` / `.tscn`,
screenshots `documentation/screenshots/c4_tunnels_*.png` (captures of the real renderer + the contact sheet).

<a id="part-env_c4_annex"></a>

## Asset manifest part -- env_c4_annex (Chapter 4 part 1, Konrad's Berlin: the tram loop and the registry annex, L_C4_Annex)

All files below are **original works generated procedurally** by the scripts in `tools/pixelgen/`
(`env_c4_annex.py` drives `env_c4_annex_loop.py` (street), `env_c4_annex_hall.py` (annex), `env_c4_annex_fx.py`
(strips), `env_c4_annex_docs.py` (inspect documents); helpers `env_c4_annex_kit.py`, `env_c4_annex_layout.py`, and
-- read-only -- `pg.py`, the tone-space painter / light bake / bitmap fonts of `env_veltrava_hall_kit.py`, the generic
painters of `env_c3p2_archive_kit.py` and the insignia drawings of `env_berlin_insignia.py`).  No pixels were copied,
traced, sampled, downloaded or AI-generated; no third-party assets, fonts or textures are embedded.

* Licence / rights: project-owned original artwork (generated for this project), freely redistributable with the
  game.  Provenance: deterministic generator (a re-run reproduces every file byte-for-byte, scene.json included;
  checked 2026-11-06), re-run with `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_c4_annex.py [--preview=DIR]`, then the shared
  `--import`.  Machine-readable list: `assets/environments/berlin_annex/provenance.json` (66 files).
* Date: 2026-11-06 (round 9).  Owner: env-annex stream.
* Palette: `annex` of data/chapters/c4.json (night, concrete, steel, fluor, dawn, paper, snow, red, skin) plus the
  proposed ramp **`lamp`** `#4A3016 #83561E #BC862C #E6B44C #FAE3A2` (sodium light at the loop, the lit windows,
  tram 74, the counter lamp -- Berlin's only warm accents; C4_DESIGN 3 lets the env streams refine the placeholder
  ramps).  Every layer, patch and document is palette-pure in it (tests/test_c4_annex_art.gd); scene.json
  `meta.palette_ramps` lists the full palette.
* **Scene A -- Wendeschleife Süd 8** (L_C4_AnnexA, Berlin, 16 Feb 1964, ~5.10, x 0..846): house No. 11, still lived
  in (Marta's window: a lamp left on behind the lace, a plant; the stairwell transom; a coal shop's shutter), house
  No. 9 gutted down to its ground floor -- the boarded double door with the red seal strip and the notice, the
  bricked-up windows, a child's sled -- and above it the exposed wall with three storeys of wallpaper, two rooms each
  (the paler ghosts of a wardrobe, a picture, the kitchen dresser, a mirror; a child's height marks on a door frame;
  a tiled stove still clinging to the wall; the stair's zig-zag), the rubble edge of the cleared lot, the tram
  shelter with the name fascia „WENDESCHLEIFE SÜD 8“ and the lit timetable case (Konrad's light table), the H sign,
  the sodium lamp under the span wire, tram 74 (two-axle motor car, lantern roof, bow collector at the contact wire,
  handwritten route board, warm interior, the open front door, fogged rear window), the lone street tree, the
  advertising column S 2 (posters, an official notice, the warm-air grate at its foot), the freight hatch under a
  hoist with the crates stencilled C-17/45, the split crate and card No. 0417 in the snow, the lorry backed up to it
  (tail lights, engine running), the site hoarding of the axis „BAUSTELLE · BETRETEN VERBOTEN“ with a red blinker;
  the wet setts of the loop with both sides of the track, slush and the lamps mirrored.  Behind: the cleared blocks
  (mid), the axis under construction with tower cranes (far), the Great Hall in the dawn haze (dome), the sky.
* **Scene B -- Nebenstelle Süd** (L_C4_AnnexB, ~6.00, x 846..1920): the cut wall of the shaft (poché with the
  draughtsman's hatch) and the ladder niche with a caged bulkhead lamp and the stencil „S 2“, the machine room
  behind an expanded-metal grille with the two fans the column breathes through, the card hall (steel card cabinets
  to the ceiling, sliding ladders, the drawer bank „HAUSHALTSKARTEI SÜD“ 1-12 with the red tab of Süd 8; Süd 3 stands a finger's width open with
  Brandt's card up — round-9 fix), the
  framed ventilation plan (Blatt 3, three shafts, three blocks hatched, „K.ADLER“), the wall clock at 6.00, the
  pneumatic-tube station „ROHRPOST“ with the capsule in the tray, the waiting bench under „WARTEN BIS ZUM AUFRUF“
  and the call number 17, the counter „AUSKUNFT · ABGLEICH“ under the plate „NEBENSTELLE SÜD“ (stamp carousel,
  stamp pad, forms, the goose-neck lamp -- the one warm light -- and the card perforator), the typists' room behind
  the glazed partition, the steel door „STAATSPOLIZEI“ with its red lamp, a fire extinguisher; air duct, tubes with
  a glass inspection length, cable tray and eight fluorescent fixtures under the slab; linoleum with the tubes'
  sheen.
* Story patches (exact crops of a layer baked with the change; gated in scene.json, applied by
  scripts/levels/c4/annex_level.gd on the registry's gate system): Konrad's tracing sheets on the timetable case
  (after c4b_solve_routes), the poster of S 2 torn back from the steel door (after c4b_inspect_column), drawer
  Süd 8 pulled out with the red C-17 tabs (after c4d_inspect_drawer), the capsule opened (after c4d_inspect_tube),
  the form on the counter (after c4d_talk_registrar).
* Insignia (TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE 12.4): `dome.png` (banners on the Great Hall: dark with the white pillar),
  `patches/notice_s2.png` (official notice on the column), `patches/plate_counter.png` and `patches/plate_stapo.png`
  are the neutral defaults with the fictional Reichssiegel; their `*_hist.png` twins carry the red banners and the
  state eagle with the wreath (drawn by env_berlin_insignia.py) as in-world iconography of the regime's offices,
  chosen at runtime by `Symbols.pick()` -- never decoration of the player's side.  The inspect documents carry no
  emblem.
* Round-9 art fixes (same generators, same palette, original and deterministic, no insignia):
  `patches/notice_9.png` (+ `_n`) -- the notice read at door_9: a typed sheet with the round red Bezirksamt stamp
  and the site plan stapled under it, pasted over the planks of the left leaf (ungated decal, scene A; the seal strip
  and the „9“ plate stay visible; painted by `env_c4_annex_loop.Street.notice9`, cfg `notice9`);
  the poster „Berlin baut“ on the shelter's right side wall (12×18 in `loop.png` at x 316–327: red title band, crane,
  dome, sun, three workers in open white shirts, the torn corner, the pencil line on the margin -- the find
  exp_shelter_poster; `Street.shelter_poster`); `props/counter_front.png` (+ `_n`) -- the counter „Auskunft ·
  Abgleich“ and what stands on it, an exact cut of `annex.png` (the pixels `Hall.counter()` paints, y ≥ 228, the form
  patch left open), a y-sorted prop with base_y 292: Fengler (feet y 276) stands behind it, the player in front.
* FX and life (looping strips, every one seamless): snow in two depths (two halves for the texture limit), warm air
  steaming out of the grate of S 2, the lorry's exhaust, chimney smoke of No. 11, the aviation lights of three cranes
  and the hoarding's blinker (1 Hz), Ilse on the last bench of tram 74 (looks out, breathes on the glass, wipes a
  peephole with her red mitten; with its own emission map), the two fans turning behind the grille, dust in their
  draught and under the tubes, a capsule shooting through the glass length of the tube every 6 s, a failing tube
  (drops out twice in 3 s), two typists behind the glass.  Ambient actors reuse existing project scenes and sprites
  by reference only: `KarskCrows` with `assets/environments/karsk_railyard/actors/crows.png` (hooded crows on the
  contact wire that lift when Konrad comes to the hatch) and `KarskBreath` with `.../breath_puff.png` (Marta's
  breath in the frost).
* Inspect documents (`assets/ui/documents/berlin_annex_*.png` + `berlin_annex_documents.json`, shown at 3x): the
  eviction notice of No. 9 with the addendum and the attached Lageplan Süd 8 (the grid and shafts of the route
  puzzle's plan), the steel door of S 2 with its enamel plate, card No. 0417 (German half, perforated edge, red
  C-17 tab), drawer Süd 8 from above (212 cards, 37 red tabs), the Lüftungsplan Blatt 3 signed „GEZ. K. ADLER“,
  the tube slip of the Staatspolizeistelle.  Story-critical words are legible pixel capitals; the rest is print /
  handwriting scribble under the UI caption; every close-up carries an alt text
  (`doc.berlin_annex_*.alt` in `data/locale/de_env_berlin_annex.json`, written by the generator; round-9 fix).  Added in the round-9 fix (compl-04, same generator
  `tools/pixelgen/env_c4_annex_docs.py`, palette `annex`): `berlin_annex_route_board.png` (320×210) — Marta's route
  board, tracing paper clipped to the cab's hardboard: line 74 in pencil with its stops and the turning loop Süd 8, the
  three dashed detours „OKT.“ „DEZ.“ „JAN.“ with hollow diamonds (the geometry of the route puzzle's trace), the depot
  stamp, the north arrow pointing left (drawn in driving direction); alt text `doc.berlin_annex_route_board.alt` in
  `data/locale/de_env_berlin_annex.json`. Original, deterministic, no insignia.
* Level script: `scripts/levels/c4/annex_level.gd` (extends the registry's gated-art script; per-scene filter
  `"scene"` / `"scene_id"`, `camera_zones` so the street never shows underground and the annex never shows the sky,
  looks with the c4.json grades `annex_loop` / `annex`, no porter radio).  Capture harness:
  `scripts/levels/c4/annex_shots.tscn` (fresh level load per scene, `--as-shots`, `--as-done`, `--symbols`).
* Screenshots: documentation/screenshots/c4_annex_contact.png (both scenes, neutral / historical symbols, story
  states), `c4_annex_a_loop_cam0000/0206.png`, `c4_annex_a_states_cam0206.png`, `c4_annex_b_annex_cam0846/1066/1280.png`,
  `c4_annex_b_states_cam1066.png`, `c4_annex_symbols_neutral_vs_hist.png`, `c4_annex_documents.png`.
* Tests: tests/test_c4_annex_art.gd (contract, files / maps / strips, insignia pairs, palette purity, hotspots at
  their objects, foreground frames clear, scene filter + camera zones + looks, story patches, memory, documents).

<a id="part-c4_cine"></a>

## Asset manifest part — c4_cine (Chapter-4 cinematics LS_C4_Homeward, _Number, _Card, _Stamp, _Voices)

* **Date:** 2026-11-06 (round 9; places-and-motion polish pass the same evening)
* **Area owner:** cine stream (C4_DESIGN.md §2.9, §10)
* **Scenes / code:** `scenes/cinematics/ch4_homeward/` (`Ch4Homeward.tscn`, `ch4_homeward.gd`), `ch4_number/`,
  `ch4_card/`, `ch4_stamp/`, `ch4_voices/`, shared `scenes/cinematics/c4_common/c4_cinematic.gd` (extends the C3P2
  base `c3p2_common/c3p2_cinematic.gd` and through it the §12.5 contract of `ch3_common/c3_cinematic.gd`: textures from
  `assets/cinematics/c4/`, `line_dur()` keeps every subtitle under 19 characters per second, the game's character
  sheets drawn by named frame ranges, the in-game stride so feet never slide, `optional_music()`; the polish pass only
  ADDED helpers: `loop_frame()` / `fx_sprite()` for the levels' fx strips, `draw_puffs()`, `draw_motes()`,
  `draw_drips()`, `draw_lit_snow()`, `par_pos()` for parallax stacks, `h01()`). Registered in `data/chapters/c4.json`
  → `cinematics`, played by `ChapterExt.play_cinematic()`. Lengths = the registered shot sheets of
  `data/v3/cinematics.json`: 14 / 13 / 11 / 11 / 24 s (unchanged).
* **Match cuts (hard cuts on shared objects, the cities in their own palettes):** the stencilled 45 of wagon C-17/45
  (cream on red-brown in Karsk → black on pine in Berlin, same shapes at the same place: one stencil generator, one
  layout entry `number.macro`); the torn perforation of card No. 0417 (German half, paper left of the edge → Russian
  half, paper right of it: one perforation geometry, mirrored); the rubber stamp (ПРИНЯТЬ violet → ABGANG red, same
  frame at the same contact point; each landing now jolts the frame by 2 px, the impressions stay put).
* **Generators:** `py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/env_c4_cine.py [--only places,homeward,number,card,stamp,voices]` writes
  every texture and `assets/cinematics/c4/layout.json` (anchors shared with the runtimes; `tests/test_c4_cinematics.gd`
  checks them). Modules: `env_c4_cine_kit.py` (palettes, stencil letters, perforation, stamps, re-lighting of the game
  sheets with their normal maps), `env_c4_cine_places.py` (build of the shared places; the round-9 painters stay for
  reference), place modules of the polish pass: `env_c4_cine_levelart.py` (reads the levels' layers and normal /
  emission maps and re-lights them like the runtime's point lights; re-bakes the street of `env_c4_annex_loop` without
  its tram), `env_c4_cine_levelplates.py` (the tram loop Süd 8 as parallax layers for dawn / morning, the level's fx
  strips, tram 3518 in both directions, the siding hall of L_C4_TunnelsB lit / dark, the booth-window view, the annex
  card hall), `env_c4_cine_yard.py` (the Karsk freight yard in parallax layers: sky, the railyard level's skyline
  re-lit for the night with the cooling towers, the far sidings, the near yard and the freight office, parapet,
  glows), `env_c4_cine_station.py` (Veltrava platform 5, ray-cast so the 56 px figures stand at their true size);
  `env_c4_cine_{homeward,number,card,stamp,voices}.py`, `env_c4_cine_figs.py` (hands and close figures; the figures
  stream). Sound: `py -3 -P tools/audiogen/c4_cine_audio.py` → `assets/audio/sfx/c4cine_*.wav` (21 files; the
  picture-synced times are constants checked by the test).
* **Figures:** close-ups (Konrad's gloves on the card, Alexei's hand on the desk, the pencil hand, Lev's hand, both
  stamp hands, Fengler at the counter, Alexei over the shoulder, Konrad from behind waving) come from the figures
  stream's `env_c4_cine_figs.py`. Every person in the wide shots is a game sheet re-lit per shot
  (`assets/characters/<id>/sheet.png` + `sheet_n.png`, 32 x 56 cells, `env_c4_cine_kit.fig_sheet`): Konrad, Alexei, Lev,
  Marta (in the tram's cab) and the platform's and the loop's people (conductor, porter, worker_a / worker_b,
  berlin_mother, berlin_child, babushka); Ilse at the tram's last window and the annex typists are the levels' own life
  strips. A character without a delivered sheet would be drawn from a declared stand-in and listed in `layout.json` →
  `standins` (currently empty).
* **Determinism:** fixed seeds everywhere; no time or global random state (level layers are read, never written).
* **Licence / provenance (all files below):** original procedural pixel art and synthesized audio made for ASHES OF
  EMPIRES: THE LAST WINTER by these generators (AI-authored code, project-owned, same licence as the game). No
  downloaded, copied or traced images, fonts or samples. Image inputs are only the project's own art: the character
  sheets and the level layers / fx strips of `assets/environments/{berlin_annex,karsk_tunnels,karsk_railyard}` (neutral
  variants only). No insignia anywhere in these films (stamps, signs, the cap badge „74“ and the forms are plain text;
  the column's notice carries the level's neutral seal), so there are no `_hist` variants and the films look the same
  with symbols on or off.
* **Texts:** memory film titles `ui.memories.film.LS_C4_*` / `film_sub.*` in `data/locale/de_c4_cine.json` (this
  stream); the films' subtitles are the story stream's `data/locale/de_c4_cinematics.json`. No disclaimers in the films.

## Asset manifest part — Chapter 4 part 1, puzzle UIs „Die Nummernkette“ and „Linie 74“ (puzzle UI stream, round 9)

Date for all entries: **2026-11-06**. Everything below is original work for this project, produced by a deterministic
generator in this repository or written by hand; no downloaded images, fonts or sounds, no AI image services, no tracing
of references. Licence: project-owned, same as the game. Contract: `documentation/C4_DESIGN.md` §2.5 (document
descriptions), §2.7 (puzzle contracts), §10.

### Puzzle art — `tools/pixelgen/gen_ui_c4.py` (`py -3 -P tools/pixelgen/gen_ui_c4.py [--preview=DIR]`)
Drawn 1:1, shown at x3 with nearest filtering (every digit stroke ≥ 3 screen px). Words are set in HD by the UI from Loc
keys; the only painted characters are the wagon numbers and Marta's pencil dates, in two pixel types designed in the
generator (plate type 7×11 with stencil bridges / brush jitter, mini type 3×5). Palette: the UI / archive ramps (paper,
ink, red, wall, amber) plus wood, rust-brown („die Farbe aller Güterwagen“), enamel blue, iron and chalk. Fixed seeds,
identical output on every run. The generator verifies the puzzle data against `data/v3/puzzles.json` before writing.

| Path | Kind | How it was made |
|---|---|---|
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c4_numbers_board.png` | slate in the siding hut: Service Tunnel 3 in chalk (freight yard fan, four switch posts with iron hooks, dashed tunnel walls, siding with buffer stop) | procedural: wood frame, dithered slate, wiped-chalk smudges, jittered chalk strokes |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c4_plate_n24318.png`, `…_c1742.png`, `…_n47702.png`, `…_c1745.png` | the four paint layers of wagon 24 318 as number plates (enamel / old stencil / brush / wet stencil) | procedural plates, numbers in the generator's own plate type |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c4_plate_<id>_lit.png` | the same plates held against the hand lamp (Gegenlicht): face as a ghost, the older number dark underneath (bare metal under 24 318) | procedural, from the same masks |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c4_num_<id>.png` | mini numbers for the wagon token's number window | procedural, mini type |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c4_wagon.png` | painted wooden token of the goods wagon (planks, sliding door, number window, wheels) | procedural |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c4_wire.png`, `c4_tick.png`, `c4_halt.png`, `c4_glow.png`, `c4_lamp_on.png`, `c4_lamp_off.png` | hanging wire, chalk tick, red chalk stop mark (ring + cross, shape-coded), banded lamp glow, hand lamp on/off | procedural |
| `assets/ui/puzzle/c4_numbers_board.json` | sizes, post / track / wagon positions, plate faces + under-layers (paint-layer chain, self-checked), measured surface colours | written by the generator |
